# Library-vocab Registry (maintained master list)

**Created:** 2026-05-29 (v2.4 dedicated Library-vocab consolidation session, post-v115 audit).
**Status:** AUTHORITATIVE. This is the **single source of truth** for Library-vocab observational items. Per routine v2.4, Library-vocab items are tracked HERE and nowhere else — per-wiki narratives may *note* an observation but must *file* it into this registry (into a cluster or as a tracked standalone). No more loose "NEW candidate X" minting in wiki narratives that never gets tracked.

**Why this file exists:** through v114 the Library-vocab "PROVISIONAL" layer was reported as "~108 items." The v2.4 consolidation established that **~108 was a phantom count** — the running *sum of per-wiki "NEW candidate" mentions*, most never tracked again, many near-duplicate or one-off. There was never a maintained list. This file replaces the phantom count with a real, maintainable registry: **8 CONFIRMED + 7 themed clusters + a short live-standalone list + a retired set.**

---

## B. Tracked clusters (7) — the new PROVISIONAL structure

Each cluster is ONE tracked entry. Members are individual observations grouped by shared structural theme. A cluster promotes toward CONFIRMED only if a coherent sub-claim reaches N=3 cross-author + cross-vertical (routine §2). New per-wiki observations file into the matching cluster rather than minting standalones.

**LV-C1 — Vendor-Direct Skill-Authoring & Distribution.** Members: **Vendor-Official-Agent-Tooling-for-Own-Product/Offering → PROMOTED to CONFIRMED Library-vocab #21 at the v151 audit (2026-06-04)** [N=3 v114 gsap library-vendor + v126 Every methodology/editorial-vendor + v149 Scrapling library-vendor; cross-author + cross-vertical + cross-scale all PASS; **promote-broad-class** chosen over split — the library-vendor (v114+v149) / methodology-editorial-vendor (v126) distinction is recorded as **internal sub-flavors inside #21**, NOT separate entries (anti-inflation: a split would create more entries, one a fresh N=1; #8's 8a/8b precedent). **This member is now promoted-out of the cluster — see §A #21.**], Vendor-Skill-Embeds-Recommendation-Bias (v114), Multi-Registry-Distribution (v89), Canonical-Snippet-Auto-Insertion (v88), Clone-as-Sibling-Directory (v88), Zero-Dependency-Bridge-Tool (v88), Progressive-Tiered-Skill-Authoring-Methodology (v121 CodexKit; 5-layer Intent/Knowledge/Execution/4C-Verification/Evolution + 3-tier T1→T2→T3 structure — filed as an ADJACENT member: third-party author, NOT vendor-direct, so it does not inflate the (a)-7-sister framing), **Commercial-AI-Startup OSS Domain-Skill-Library as Brand/Community-Infrastructure + Companion-App Funnel (v124 K-Dense scientific-agent-skills — ADJACENT member: distinct from v114 product-vendor-for-own-product because the skills are general-domain scientific skills, and the company monetizes via a *separate* hosted platform + a *free OSS* BYOK desktop app; the OSS library is brand/community-infrastructure + top-of-funnel, not a product-usage manual)**. *Theme:* how skills/tools are authored + pushed into agents. (a)-7-sister framing for the v114 vendor-direct item lives here. Cluster status: live, N=watch.

**LV-C2 — Cost / Capacity Economics of Free-or-Cheap Inference.** Members: Free-Tier-Stacking-as-Capacity-Aggregation (v112), Cost-Attribution-via-LiteLLM (v89), Cost-Attribution-via-External-Pricing-Service (v109), Quota-Arbitrage-Across-Vendor-Internal-Surfaces (v67), Sticky-Session-Model-Affinity (v112), **API-Relay/Middle-Layer-Reseller-Economy (v117 CodexPlusPlus; JOJO Code/AIGoCode/PackyCode sponsor a config-switcher that points a coding agent at relay endpoints — filed at v120 audit)**. *Promotable sub-claim:* **"Cost-Attribution-via-External-Service" N=2** (LiteLLM v89 + pricing-service v109) → PROMOTION-ELIGIBLE at N=3 (NOT advanced by v117 — relay-reseller-economy is a distinct sub-theme, not cost-attribution). (#13 OSS-with-hosted-Pro-SaaS-tier-on-MIT-base = RETIRED v96; historical member.)

**LV-C3 — Doc-Coherence: Anti-Drift vs Drift-Exhibited.** Members: "names-the-drift-and-drifts-anyway" inverse-pole **N=2** (v66 agentmemory + v113 ai-engineering-from-scratch; sub-observation of Pattern #81), **Self-Referential-Methodology-Demonstration N=3 (v87 + v126 compound-engineering-plugin [in-repo `docs/brainstorms/` + `docs/plans/` dogfooding its Brainstorm→Plan→Compound loop] + v131 harness [`_workspace/` auditor/content/scout/strategist team that audited+planned harness's OWN launch, following harness's own `{phase}_{agent}_{artifact}` output convention]; promotion-eligible at next audit ~v134-v135. ⚠️ v131 is a SOFT 3rd instance — the `_workspace/` artifacts are NOT explicitly stamped "generated by harness"; structural evidence only [naming-convention match to SKILL.md Phase 5-1 + harness-as-its-own-subject]. Audit may keep N=3 or downgrade to N=2-plus-adjacent.)**, Marketing-Site-vs-README-Divergence (v80), **skill-count-drift-with-spec/security-CI-but-no-count-CI (v124 scientific-agent-skills — 141↔142 across opening/What's-Included/badge; WEAK member, does NOT advance the N=2 inverse-pole to N=3 — a minor count-inconsistency, not a load-bearing manifest drift)**. *Cross-ref:* Pattern #81-proper (Manifest-Drift-as-First-Class-CI-Concern, v64 claude-seo) is the opposite pole and lives at the Pattern layer. Cluster status: live; the N=2 inverse-pole is the strongest member (N=3 watch).

**LV-C4 — Cadence / Velocity micro-signals.** Members: High-Development-Cadence-relative-to-Star-Velocity **N=2** (v89+v107), High-Release-Cadence-at-Micro-Scale (v89), Release-Cadence-Discipline (v88), High-Fork-Ratio-at-Micro-Scale-with-Sustained-Dev (v121 CodexKit; 0.526 fork-ratio at 19★ + 8 releases — explicitly distinct from CONFIRMED #17 Try-Once-and-Move-On, which lacks sustained dev; here the high fork-ratio coexists with active maintenance). *Cross-ref:* Pattern #52 (velocity sub-classes) + #16/#17 (CONFIRMED) cover the headline engagement/velocity vocabulary; this cluster holds the residual cadence one-offs. Status: live, N=watch.

**LV-C5 — Design-Skill Composition Vocabulary.** Members: Compositional-Mix-&-Match (v87), Universal-Modifier-Composable (v87), Multi-Attribute-Style-Schema (v87), Visual-Directions-Taxonomy **N=2** (v81+v83), CO-EXISTENT-framing-axes **N=2** (v82+v83). *Theme:* design-skill-local composition vocabulary. Largely vertical-bound (design-skill cluster); unlikely to cross-promote. Status: live but vertical-capped.

**LV-C6 — Session / Prompt-Persistence & Autonomy.** Members: Tri-Layer-Prompt-Persistence (v88), Local-Conversation-Plus-Bridge-Summaries (v88), Auto-Execution-Mode-Prerequisite (v88), Autonomy-vs-Session-Handoff-Continuum (v79). *Theme:* teleport/harness-local session+autonomy vocabulary. Status: live, N=watch.

**LV-C7 — Packaging / Runtime-Substrate one-offs.** Members: Single-Tool-No-Fallback-Packaging (v79), Rust-for-Observability-Perf (v89), Bun-primary (v84, OVERRIDE-caveat), **Tauri-Desktop Management-GUI for a Coding Agent N=3 (v73 cc-switch + v117 CodexPlusPlus + v153 ai-switcher — Tauri desktop control-planes that manage *another* coding agent's config/providers/accounts; v73+v117 filed at v120 audit, v153 ai-switcher filed at ship 2026-06-04 = the genuine 3rd management-GUI [native-macOS account/quota control-plane for Claude Code + Codex + Antigravity])**. *v120 audit note (honest downgrade):* the ship-time "LV-C7 Tauri-desktop-harness → N=3 (incl. v118 OpenHuman)" claim is **CORRECTED to N=2** — v118 OpenHuman is a Tauri desktop app but a **standalone agent assistant**, NOT a management-GUI-for-another-agent, so it is **adjacent, not a clean 3rd instance**; the "archetype-vs-Tauri-stack-popularity" check (flagged at v117/v118) resolves toward *don't promote* — "uses Tauri" is incidental; the tight claim is the management-GUI-for-another-agent shape (v73+v117 only). *Cross-ref:* Pattern #84 84d Hardware-Substrate-Tolerance (CONFIRMED N=4) covers the structural substrate-tolerance claim. Status: live (the non-management-GUI one-offs remain in this cluster); **the management-GUI sub-claim was PROMOTED-OUT to CONFIRMED Library-vocab #22 at the v158 audit (2026-06-04→05)** [N=3 v73 cc-switch + v117 CodexPlusPlus + v153 ai-switcher; broad-class promotion, sub-flavors recorded inside #22; see §A]. **This member is now promoted-out of the cluster — see §A #22.**

| **C33** · **Text-Space Skill Optimizer — Training-Loop Optimization of a Frozen Agent's Natural-Language Skill Document via Validation-Gated Edits** | **N=1** | **v178 SkillOpt (`microsoft` / Microsoft Research)** | **filed v178; CORPUS-FIRST — a tool/methodology that treats an agent's natural-language SKILL DOCUMENT as the *trainable state* of a frozen model and improves it with a deep-learning-style training loop in TEXT space (no weight updates): a separate optimizer model turns scored rollouts into bounded add/delete/replace edits, gated by a textual learning-rate budget, and a candidate edit is ACCEPTED ONLY IF it strictly improves a held-out validation score (loop = rollout→reflect→edit→validate; rejected-edit buffer; epoch-wise slow/meta update; deployable best_skill.md ≈300–2,000 tok; zero inference-time overhead). servo / Verso / Ladybird precede as engine efforts. ⭐⭐⭐ **The v267 headline is that Obscura IMPLEMENTS Lightpanda's vendor namespace and never names it** — `crates/obscura-cdp/src/domains/lp.rs:13` implements `getMarkdown`, `README.md:403` publishes the row `| **LP** | getMarkdown |` with the initials expanded nowhere, and a grep for `lightpanda` across every `.rs`/`.md`/`.toml`/`.yml` returns **zero**. **No code derivation alleged or evidenced** (Zig→Rust; `lp.rs` is ~20 lines calling Obscura's own `HTML_TO_MARKDOWN_JS`; implementing a protocol method name is compatibility, not copying, and there is **no licence issue**) — the finding is **legibility**, and it matters because the two projects differ on **AGPL-3.0 vs Apache-2.0**, the single most decision-relevant fact for anyone choosing between them. **CAPABILITY-not-form-factor, cleared deliberately:** the engine choice sets the resource envelope (~30 MB vs 200 MB+ page-stated), which is what makes one browser per agent task viable at concurrency — the **v244 OpenSandbox** (execution substrate) / **v215 grok-build** reasoning, and what separates this from the declines at **v222 lobehub** (world-canonical ≠ world-first, fame ≠ mint) · **v236 dsh-TUI** (form factor within a genre) · **v211 PixelRAG** (technique) · **v210 AIRI** / **v196 meetily** (domain-not-capability). Mint-at-N=1-for-a-corpus-first-capability-scoped-not-world-first is settled precedent (v206/v207/v215/v221/v223/v224/v233/v244). ⚠️ **NO-MINT ALTERNATIVE RECORDED (audit-reviewable):** that Obscura is merely the **second entrant in Lightpanda's already-named class**, so the honest act is a corpus-knowledge data-point rather than a row. **It loses because outside-corpus prior art does not create a corpus N=2** — the **camofox v179** precedent exactly (*"Camoufox not a corpus subject; mentions ≠ recursion"*) — which is what makes this "corpus-first, not world-first" rather than N=2. ⭐ **Scope note for a future N=2:** the class is **owning the rendering/layout pipeline**, NOT merely "a fast headless browser" — a candidate must implement its own cascade and layout, so Lightpanda itself would qualify (and is the obvious ~v268 subject) while a Chromium-flag-tuned wrapper would not. **DISTINCT from C34 anti-detect/stealth browser (HELD at N=2 this ship):** C34 requires *"a browser whose PRIMARY reason to exist is fingerprint evasion"*, and Obscura's stealth is an **opt-in cargo feature** (`crates/obscura-cli/Cargo.toml:17` — `default = []`) absent from the default release archive, one row of seven in its own comparison table ⇒ Obscura is precisely the *"general browser-automation tool that treats stealth as one feature"* C34 defines itself against. SECONDARY scoped (NOT this row): **#18 B1-MCP** ≈N=15→≈N=16 (first-party 37-tool MCP server) · **#66 supply-chain the corpus's strongest POSITIVE exemplar** with pi v228 (12/12 GitHub Actions SHA-pinned, read-only tokens, security policy fetched from the trusted base so a PR cannot weaken the check judging it, PR caches restored-never-saved, 0 git deps, reasoned `deny.toml`) · **#83 SPLIT** (honest: "may differ from Chromium", "not rustfmt-clean", ±10% noise floor, a CORS comment naming its own attack; not honest: four mutually-inconsistent speed figures + "Anti-detect: Built-in") · **#19 19a**. NON-claims: **NOT CONFIRMED Library-vocab #24** (requires a product *"whose primary function is not being an agent tool"*; Obscura's primary function IS agent infrastructure — clean discrimination) · **NOT #52** (stars/Trendshift page-stated, §37.4) · **NOT #57** (no corpus subject is a dependency; Lightpanda is not a corpus subject) · **NOT a new top-level pattern** (max #85). |

## E. Standing anti-re-accumulation rules (routine v2.4)

1. **Single registry.** This file is the only tracked location. Wiki narratives may NOTE an observation but must FILE it here (cluster or standalone) or omit it.
2. **Registration cap.** A wiki may register at most **2 genuinely-NEW standalone** Library-vocab candidates; further observations must join an existing cluster or be omitted. (Formalizes the v115 freeze.)
3. **Auto-retire (time-aware — AMENDED v151 audit 2026-06-04, operator sign-off).** A standalone N=1 is auto-retired at the next audit only once it has gone **both ≥15 wikis AND ≥30 days** without strengthening ("15 wikis OR 30 days, whichever is longer" — retire on the threshold satisfied later). Mechanically checked against this file. The 30-day floor protects recent items during burst-shipping; the 15-wiki ceiling still retires genuinely-stale items under slow cadence. Re-registerable on a genuine 2nd instance. (Routine: `v2.4 §28.3` + `v2.6 §39`.)
4. **Count discipline.** Report the **tracked-registry count** (8 CONFIRMED + 7 clusters + N live standalones), NOT the cumulative-mention sum. The "~108" figure is officially deprecated as a non-maintenance artifact.
5. **Filing is an ACT, not a claim (NEW, v120 audit).** "File it into the registry" means **edit this file in the same session as the wiki ship** — not write "filed into LV-Cx" in the wiki narrative and move on. **v120 audit FINDING:** v116–v119 narratives *claimed* to file observations (LV-C2 relay-economy, LV-C7 Tauri-desktop) but the registry was **never actually edited** — the exact "note that never gets tracked" failure mode §28 exists to prevent, reproduced one level up. The cap (rule 2) and count-discipline (rule 4) held; the filing step did not. Remediated at the v120 audit (the v116–v119 observations are now actually filed above). Going forward: no wiki ship is "done" until its registry observations are written into this file.

**v194 fff (2026-07-04) — +1 §C standalone (CORPUS-FIRST for the surface, N=1):** "Resident In-Process Fuzzy File+Content Search Library for AI Coding Agents" (anchor `dmtrKovalenko/fff`, author Dmitriy Kovalenko — independent perf-focused OSS dev, confirmed date-io/odiff/fframes, member of LightSourceAI, NOT Anthropic). A resident, in-process Rust file-search LIBRARY (explicitly not a CLI) — typo-resistant fuzzy path + content search, frecency ranking, background watcher, in-memory content index — delivered to agents via an MCP server (`find_files`/`grep`/`multi_grep`) + Pi extension + Neovim plugin + C/Node/Bun/Python SDKs. A locally-run proxy that calls Gemini's **internal `batchexecute` RPC** (`https://gemini.google.com{prefix}/_/BardChatUi/data/assistant.lamda.BardFrontendService/StreamGenerate`) with a **reimplemented `SAPISIDHASH` Authorization header**, parses the wrapped `wrb.fr` nested-JSON stream, and re-exposes it as `/v1/chat/completions` + `/v1/models` + **`/v1/responses` (Codex CLI)** + **`/v1beta/models…` (Gemini CLI)** — with tool calling, streaming, multimodal input. **Pure HTTP — no browser, no Playwright, no Selenium** (mimicry is header-level only: spoofed UA + `X-Same-Domain: 1` + Referer/Origin). MIT; Python; `requires-python >=3.8`; **ZERO required dependencies** (optional `streaming` extra = `httpx>=0.25`) → runs on the standard library; v1.1.0 in `pyproject.toml` but **0 releases published**; ~2.7k★/~607–627 forks/59 commits/6 contributors page-stated §37.4 → **NOT #52**; Trendshift #42981; ships an 8-module package (`config/gemini/models/multimodal/server/tools`) **and** a 1,108-line self-contained `gemini_web2api.py`, plus `tests/`, a `docker.yml`-only CI, a Cloudflare Worker, and a `gemini-cookie-sync-extension` that harvests the `SNlM0e` XSRF token + `gemini_bl` build ID from a live session. **Anonymous access works for the Flash-tier models with NO Google account at all** (`gemini-3.1-pro` silently routes to Flash without a paid-subscription cookie) — a purer free-tier harvest than v231, and an inverted risk profile (nothing of yours to ban; pressure lands on Google's IP throttling, which the Cloudflare edge path exists to spread). that row already distinguishes v140's tool-registration gating) · **v223 openinterpreter §C** (harness **emulation** — swaps whole harness personalities; v238 is one measured lever inside one harness) · **v178 SkillOpt §C** (optimises a skill **document** in text space via a validation gate — different object) · **v168 ponytail §C** (a hand-authored behaviour ruleset **injected** as prose; v238 **removes**, and its lever is the API tool schema) · **v186 DeepSpec** (speculative decoding — weights/decoding layer, not the request surface). **DECLINED on five grounds: (1) emphatically NOT world-first — Anthropic ships the mechanism first-party** (the **Tool Search Tool with `defer_loading: true` STRIPS deferred tools from the initial system prompt** and restores them on demand via a search tool, documented **77K → 8.7K tokens** on request #1 = the same bootstrap→discovery→promote shape), **and the literature is thick** (arXiv:2604.21816 dynamic tool gating + lazy schema loading, Apr 2026 — explicitly tying tool-visibility minimisation to preserved reasoning quality; arXiv:2605.24660 *"How Many Tools Should an LLM Agent See?"*; arXiv:2512.15274 *"Well Begun, Half Done"*; arXiv:2506.22058 *"Lost at the Beginning of Reasoning"*; plus a least-privilege tool-restriction literature framed as security); **EVIDENCE STATUS (load-bearing, recorded because any future use must carry it):** **ESTABLISHED** — the preset reliably changes the *style* of the model's first reasoning chain (#65 @TipsyDrifter, 3×3 randomised block: **9/9 separated perfectly by preset**, *"real and very robust"*); and auto-injected context can **destroy** the effect (#6 @slicenferqin: **~9 KB** skill catalog → **0/9** vs ~81% without, which explains why third parties with local skills *"could never replicate"*). **NOT ESTABLISHED** — that it makes the model *better*: #65 ability **+3.3, 95% CI [−2.6, +9.3]**, ANOVA F(2,6)=1.05, **not significant**. **DISPUTED** — #51 (@JimMilk, 11 rounds × 3 OSes): Ability **85–90, 98/99 NOT reproduced**, trajectory showed **no correlation with scores**, *"not reproducible on current weights"*, losses traced to a **model-layer defect** (F3-05 session_id implicit authorization) *"unrelated to OS/preset/trajectory."* **RETRACTED** — #60 (@MolecularFullerene, a **third party**) showed the headline scores had been **retroactively re-attributed** (those runs used `pwsh`+`read` → the full 25-tool catalog; the exact Minimal pair came later, commit `a1e1c1d`); the author accepted it and the README carries the caveat, **but the repo TAGLINE still advertises "Project2 98/99."** **The two independent replications disagree with each other.**

**SECONDARY (NOT minted):** **⭐⭐ Pattern #83 honest-deficiency-disclosure — the corpus's sharpest instance to date AND a STRONGER FORM than self-disclosure: author-accepted THIRD-PARTY PROVENANCE CORRECTION** (#60 forced the fix; the author documented the caveat, added that the generic prefab *"must not"* carry those scores, and links both failing replications — while the tagline still advertises the retracted number) → a possible new 83 sub-mechanism, **for the audit to decide**. · **⭐ LV-C2 cost-economics — a research project KILLED BY INFERENCE PRICING**: eval rounds went **~¥2 → ~¥12 (~$0.28 → ~$1.70)** and `FAREWELL.md` (2026-08-17) states 「继续在没有验证能力的情况下堆功能，是对用户不负责任」 (*"continuing to pile on features without verification capability is irresponsible to users"*) → the citable lesson: **for inference-time research the binding constraint is the cost of EVALUATION, not development** (a tiny absolute number × many required rounds = project death). · **#20 Token-Economy-Quantification QUALIFIED-ADJACENT, NO N-bump** — it quantifies the intervention's *behavioural* effect (we/let-me rates, hit rates, ablations), not artifact token footprint → **N stays 4** (the ponytail v168 handling). · **#57 recorded, N NOT incremented** — the **FOURTH consecutive ship in one dependency graph** and a **third structural kind of DSH extension** (v235 host → v236 UI-seam replacement → v237 UI-layer plugin-as-platform → **v238 agent preset / row composition**); treat output as untrusted"*), and the README's own *"same trust level as shell access."* · **Community collective, not a solo build** — `ACKNOWLEDGEMENTS.md` credits ~15 handles; **two of the seven shipped modes (Eternal Minimal, Wire Think-Execute) were designed by Greenhand-monster**, and the FAREWELL states contributors' work *"clearly exceed[s] my own capability range."* · **DSH ecosystem formation** — directories now claim **1,253** (dshplugin.dev) / **1,345** (deepseekdocs) plugins vs **v236's ~1,120 one day earlier**; — **§C-MINT ALTERNATIVE RECORDED + DECLINED:** *"Container-as-Plugin Desktop Shell — an installable desktop application that composes its own window/tray/terminal/update shell as an ordinary plugin of the agent runtime it launches, with the runtime pinned as an unmodified submodule behind a CI-enforced published-package boundary."* **Five grounds:** (1) ⭐ **decisive external prior art, verified not assumed — Eclipse RCP/OSGi**, whose official plug-in-architecture article names *the workbench UI itself* as a self-extending plug-in contributing to its own extension points (Eclipse 1.0 2001-11-29; 3.0 2004-06-21 = platform as OSGi bundles), with **JupyterLab/lumino** (shell provided by a plugin) and **Theia** (DI-composed core) populating the space; adversarial verdict **REFUTED**; (2) ⭐⭐ **the subject does NOT claim novelty** — its own `dsh-community-fabric/docs/research/` holds `mature-plugin-frameworks.md` **and `vscode-extension-model.md`**, studying Eclipse/Chrome/VS Code, so 「桌面本身也是「插件」」 is a **design commitment, not a priority claim**; minting would over-claim *on the subject's behalf* (the **v211 PixelRAG** discipline); (3) **form-factor-within-genre, ruled on twice** (**hermes-webui v227** DECLINED; **lobehub v222** decisive; + **Codex-Dream-Skin v216** — a UI layer for a rival's tool is not a mintable capability class, §C vocab is capability-shaped); (4) **§28** + the **DSH chain now EIGHT deep** (v216→v222→v227→v236→v237→v239→v240→v241) and **seven consecutive DSH-ecosystem ships** (v235→v241), seven consecutive NO-MINTs; (5) **packaging/domain-not-capability** (meetily v196 / TimesFM v193 / AIRI v210) → a **DEFERRED watch axis at N=1**, credit-on-next-instance. ⚠️ An agent's "NOVEL" verdict was **rejected** — it asserted *"Eclipse RCP ❌ NO"* against a verbatim primary-source quote and cited nothing.

— **⭐⭐⭐ THE SHIP'S HEADLINE — A HARD CORRECTION TO v240's OWN METHOD.** v240's celebrated headline was *"provenance measured without the mocked API — a clone carries authoritative git metadata."* **That holds only when the history belongs to the subject.** Here the clone reports **12,637 commits (44.7% merges), ~69 days old, 52 author emails, top author 41%, and 2,219 commits from 10 distinct `@deepseek.com` addresses (21.1% of non-merge); ZERO signed** — all true of the **repository**, all false of the **product**. **PROOF:** ONE root commit (2026-06-10) whose first five messages are *"microkernel architecture"* → *"Yarn 4 workspaces"* → **"Vendor Cordis framework packages as source"** (**DSH itself being born**); **`packages/`, a path that no longer exists, carries 8,103 commits**; **915** merges "from `deepseek-harness/`" + 69 "from `deepseek-ai/`" vs **11** from "`anywhere-labs/`"; the deleted history contains upstream's OWN architecture notes; and **the pivot `4e3eb91` (2026-08-15 01:07:13, `t4wefan`, "refactor(repo): isolate upstream and desktop workspace") DELETED 7,405 FILES** while creating `.gitmodules` + `dsh-plugin-desktop/`. **THE PRODUCT: 343 commits (307 non-merge) over ~3.8 days, ~6 authors, ZERO `@deepseek.com` commits post-pivot** → ⚠️ **this REVERSES my own initial "affiliation tension" framing and EXONERATES the project** (the 「并非 DeepSeek 官方产品」 disclaimer is **accurate**; the repo advertises no commit count anywhere). **THE RULE: a clone's git metadata is authoritative about the REPOSITORY, never the PROJECT; where history is inherited, measure provenance FROM THE DIVERGENCE POINT. DETECTOR: heavy-history paths that no longer exist in the tree; root-commit count; merge subjects naming foreign orgs.** ⭐ **v240: "cannot see what is MISSING from the source." v241: "cannot see WHERE THE SUBJECT BEGAN."** ⚠️ **BOTH assigned agents FAILED** — the reader self-contradictory (25+22+3 ≠ 12,637), the **adversarial verifier CONFIRMED the wrong framing** with correct arithmetic → **a verifier checks the claim you hand it; it does not reframe the question** (a new method lesson, D21).

So the transition is **deliberate and mechanically enforced as a state machine** — better than v240's category check — and **the one stale artifact is the file the AI agents read.** ⭐⭐ **The vault application is exact: `CLAUDE.md` IS an `AGENTS.md`, and `_state/03c-projects-v61-v183.md` holds entries through v241 under a `-v183` label.**

### §F — v242 deepseek-harness (CORPUS-RECURSIVE REVISIT of v235) — 2026-08-18 — **NO MINT**

**Subject:** `deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness` (`dsh`), MIT, `0.1.0-rc.7`, clone HEAD `99f6f02fecdb…` — **the same repository as v235**, re-analysed **from a full source clone** (the gap v235 disclosed in bold: *"⚠️ NOT source-cloned"*). — **The mint was tested ADVERSARIALLY and REFUTED on three grounds.** (1) **Decisive external prior art** — Eclipse RCP/OSGi (Eclipse 1.0 2001; 3.0 2004 = platform as OSGi bundles), the canonical self-extending plugin architecture whose workbench is itself a plug-in; VS Code's extension host, JupyterLab/lumino, Theia populate the space — the same ground v241's desktop mint was declined on. (2) ⭐⭐ **The subject claims novelty NOWHERE and credits the architecture to a THIRD PARTY** — `README.md:7`: *"It uses an architecture where **everything is a plugin**, and is powered by [Cordis](https://github.com/cordiverse/cordis)…"*; `vendor/cordis/LICENSE` = *"Copyright (c) 2021-present Shigma"*, upstream `cordiverse/cordis`. **dsh is the CONSUMER of the plugin framework, not its author** → minting would over-claim **on the subject's behalf** (the **v211** discipline). (3) **§28 anti-inflation + chain depth nine deep** — a revisit of the chain's own host is the last place to mint.

— **⭐⭐ FLAGGED TO THE OVERDUE AUDIT — the stale v140 §C row "Graduated / Least-Privilege Tool-Exposure" now has an *IMPLEMENTED* N=2 candidate.** That row has sat at **N=1 for ~100 wikis**; **v238** raised it; **v241** declined Fabric's version because enforcement was *disclosed unimplemented*. DSH's is implemented and enforced by composition — `docs/tool-catalog.md` on `@deepseek-ai/dsh-tool-cordis`: *"**Not in any shipped tree (a deliberate opt-in — dynamic package code reaches the real runtime**)… injects `ctx.dynamicCordisRunner`… **a composition missing it never activates the tools**"*; same discipline on the terminal, schedule and session-query toolsets. **RECORDED, NOT SELF-EXECUTED — a promotion is an audit act** (the v232 rule, reaffirmed v235/v241). Decide alongside v238's and v241's claims on the same row.

— **Instance-strengthening, RECORDED and NOT self-incremented.** **pi v228's supply-chain exemplar → an independent instance**: pnpm `strictDepBuilds` + `allowBuilds` **deny-by-default, 5 allowed / 3 explicitly DENIED with written reasons**, plus a **`minimumReleaseAge` cooldown whose headline named exclusion is `@earendil-works/pi-ai@0.82.1` — the corpus subject dsh depends on**; structurally the **same 5-allow/3-deny shape v241 found in the desktop client** under a different package manager → host + client + pi as an independent set. **#57** — the pi dependency is now **exactly version-pinned and named in a release-age exclusion list**, the most precisely-evidenced form the corpus holds, plus a **six-way governance fan-in newly *explained* rather than observed**. **#83** two-sided (telemetry off-switch scope disclosed honestly; `token-meter` self-labelled approximate). **#66** two-sided (exemplary vendoring + mirror-free lockfile vs. unvetted plugin ecosystem + default-ON telemetry). **grok-build v215 §C row 102 N=2 — UNCHANGED from v235: recorded, NOT executed.**

— **⭐⭐⭐ THE SHIP'S HEADLINE (governance, not pattern):** `CONTRIBUTING.md` — *"**We are sorry that we cannot accept external pull requests at the moment**"* → *"Create a plugin… **Associate your GitHub project with the `dsh-plugin` topic**"* → *"an idea, an official showcase, and a source of inspiration, **but not a mandate from us**"* — **is the governance cause of the entire six-ship DSH periphery.** v236/v237/v239 (plugins), v240 (a 1,390-entry catalogue indexing **the very topic named at `CONTRIBUTING.md:15`**), v241 (a client containing the host) all descend from it. **1,008 PR merges, 939 from `deepseek-harness/…` + 69 from `deepseek-ai/…`, all internal; `github.com/deepseek-harness` is a real org with ZERO public repositories.** "Everything is a plugin" is an architecture **and** a contribution policy.

— **⭐⭐⭐ THREE NEW RULES, all about the vault's own method.** **D25 — an un-cloned subject's caveats are hearsay:** four of v235's five "load-bearing" caveats were inherited third-party claims and three fail on contact with source (squashed-merge/contributor-hostile **REFUTED**; "~19% compatibility (41/219)" **no in-repo basis**; "~10× tokens" **no in-repo basis**, and the project's own meter is a self-declared ~4-char-per-token heuristic; "a confirmed context-duplication bug" **documented as fixed 2026-08-11, before the rc.5 v235 rated**) — while **the one that holds is the one v235 derived from source itself** (no eval harness; `BENCHMARK.md` still 3 lines, **adversarially CONFIRMED**). **D23 — every count over files or lines must state its language basis:** v235's *"1,386 decision records"* = 693 EN + 692 ZH; **real = 693** (516 implemented / 143 archived / 25 proposed / 11 rejected); *"~170K lines of docs"* = 170,752 all-`.md` but **90,857 English**. ⭐ The subject enforces the pairing with a **custom git merge driver** (*"DeepSeek Harness bilingual pairing records"*) while the vault counted the translations — and **v241 had documented the very convention** (12 notes / 36 files) one ship *after* the error it would have prevented; an independent agent this run fell in the same hole (*"1,389"*). **D24 — a version-number delta is not a code delta:** **rc.5 → rc.6 = 69 MINUTES, +224/−224, version strings only**, so v236's/v237's `^rc.6` pins recorded as *"one prerelease ahead"* are **69 minutes ahead across zero code**; rc.6 → rc.7 = 4 days, **zero breaking changes, zero package delta (219 both ends)**.

— **⭐⭐ THE STACK-WIDE GAP the chain had been circling:** the host ships **816 spec files / ~299K lines of test code against ~245K of source / 723 fixtures / 4 numbered postmortems / 11 recorded *rejected* decisions** — and **no evaluation harness at all**. With v240 (catalogue checks metadata) and v241 (installer checks identity, verbatim *"they do not review the plugin or its dependency tree for malicious or unsafe behavior"*): **nothing anywhere in this stack measures whether a plugin makes the agent *worse*. Identity is verified end to end; EFFECT is unmeasured end to end** — and the only party positioned to measure it is the one that closed its PR queue.

— **⭐ Cross-verification of the vault's own prior ship:** this clone's HEAD `99f6f02fecdb…` **is exactly the submodule pin v241 recorded**, and the root commit `b67e81ac976…` (2026-06-10, 48 insertions) is **the same commit v241 found at the base of the desktop repo** → **v241's D19 closed from the origin side: that history belongs here.** Provenance hand-verified: 12,404 commits · 1 root · ~68 days · 42 authors · **2,246 (18.1%) from 10 `@deepseek.com` addresses, spread across the project's own development, NOT inherited** · **ZERO of 12,404 GPG-signed**. ⚠️ **Epistemic boundary (adversarially confirmed): the 42.4% top author CANNOT be confirmed as staff from source** (GitHub noreply masks the domain; no CONTRIBUTORS file; org membership is external metadata and **the API is mocked, §37.4**) → UNKNOWN, not inferred.

— **⭐ A provenance FORM the corpus hadn't recorded:** of 1,008 PR merges, **`worktree/` 216 and `codex/` 211 vs `claude/` 3** — AI-tool provenance encoded in the **branch name**, surviving permanently in every merge-commit subject; **more durable than v239's PR-form field**, though incidental rather than deliberate. ⚠️ A prefix marks branch tooling, **not** authorship of the code — no claim that 20.9% of the code is AI-written. **And DeepSeek's own harness was substantially developed through OpenAI's Codex CLI while shipping Claude Code as a delegatable subagent** (rc.7 extends it: `feat(agent-presets): enable background Codex and Claude Code subagent tasks`).

**§F NO-MINT LOG — v243 ToolJet (2026-08-19).** `ToolJet/ToolJet` — an AGPL-3.0 open-source low-code platform for internal tools/dashboards/workflows, self-branded *"the open-source foundation of ToolJet AI"*; ToolJet Solutions Inc; ✅ **SOURCE-CLONED** (working tree at HEAD `2f7638c2d` + full 17,101-commit blobless history). GOAL-ALIGNED INCLUDE 3/4 [(a) FAIL · (b) STRONG ⚠️MODERATE-reviewable · (c) STRONG · (d) STRONG], rated on the **v213 geti precedent** (a product platform admitted on the strength of the agent tooling it ships). **NO MINT. Counts 46/11 UNCHANGED; §C live standalones 49 unchanged; surface ≈56 unchanged.**

**Declined on four independent grounds.** (1) **Low-code / internal-tool-builder class — decisive prior art**: Microsoft Access 1992 / Power Apps ~2015 · Salesforce Force.com ~2000 · Oracle APEX ~2003 · Zoho Creator ~2006 · **Retool 2017** · **Budibase 2019-20** · **Appsmith 2020**, all preceding ToolJet's verified root commit **2021-03-31**; corpus-first for a **DOMAIN** is not mintable (the meetily v196 / TimesFM v193 / mlsysbook v197 discipline) and **fame is not a mint** (lobehub v222, decisive). (2) **"AI generates the internal app from a prompt" — 18+ months late**: Retool AI, Power Apps Copilot, Vercel v0, Appsmith AI, Glide AI all **2023**; bolt.new + Lovable **2024**; ToolJet AI **Feb 2025** — and the corpus already opened that cluster at **v224 open-lovable**, itself declined as not-world-first. (3) **The `AGENTS.md`-canonical + `CLAUDE.md`-symlink convention is not ToolJet's** — a multi-vendor community convention predating its adoption, claimed nowhere in the repo. (4) **The 905 `Co-Authored-By: Claude` trailers are a Claude Code DEFAULT** — zero references to any trailer exist across `.agents/`, `.claude/`, all 13 `AGENTS.md`, `.github/`, `.husky/`, `.gitconfig` or `CONTRIBUTING.md`, so no provenance-practice mint is available (**an initial contrary framing by the analyst was withdrawn**). Plus **§28** anti-inflation and the **v211** corpus-first-technique-is-not-a-capability-class discipline.

**⭐ INSTANCE-STRENGTHENING RECORDED, NOT EXECUTED — FLAGGED TO THE AUDIT.** **v213 (Intel geti) committed cross-harness agent-config symlinks → N=1 → N=2.** ToolJet is a genuine **cross-author, cross-domain, NON-PORT** second instance: `AGENTS.md` is the real file and **`CLAUDE.md` is a 9-byte filesystem symlink to it** at three levels, with `.claude/skills/{commit,merge,create-pr}` symlinked to `../../.agents/skills/{…}`. Per the clean-2nd-instance precedent (camofox v179 / codebase-memory-mcp v172 / Strix v190 / OmniRoute v208) this is **recorded, not self-promoted — a promotion is an audit act (the v232 rule).** ⚠️ **The audit should weigh a mechanical difference between the two instances:** geti symlinked a **skill suite**; ToolJet symlinks a skill directory **and the root context file**, and the root-context-file half is the load-bearing one (it is what makes drift between `CLAUDE.md` and `AGENTS.md` *impossible* rather than merely discouraged). ⚠️ Also note the instance is **one day old on `main`** (authored 2026-07-28, squash-merged 2026-08-18) — durability is unproven.

**Other axes touched, no N self-incremented.** **#83** honest-deficiency disclosure ×2 — `.env.example` documents `TOOLJET_WORKFLOW_SANDBOX_BYPASS` *with its own warning* (*"Python code will run without isolation when bypassed"*), and `AGENTS.md` discloses that the pre-commit hook lints frontend files only. **#66** supply-chain **positive exemplar** — **exactly 1 lifecycle script across 103 tracked `package.json` files** (the root's `husky install`; zero `postinstall`/`preinstall`) and **zero lockfile mirrors**, joining **pi v228** and the v241/v242 pair. **v184 (Osmani) / v189 (loop-engineering) grill→PRD→AFK→QA pipeline** — `plans/widget-css-class.md` carries `(Grill correction — …)`, `(PM correction, 2026-06-18)` and `Type: AFK`: **the corpus's first sighting of that pipeline's artifacts committed inside a production repository**, recorded as an **independent convergence, NOT a #57 dependency**. ⚠️ **NO #57 claim** — the `.gitignore` names `docs/superpowers/` and `.codegraph/`, but `.codegraph/` was **not verified** to be corpus subject v70 and a directory name is not evidence.

**⚠️ A prior-art claim was DISCARDED as confabulation:** a research agent asserted that *"the AGENTS.md format was originally developed by Anthropic … definitely before ToolJet (2021)."* AGENTS.md did not exist in 2021, and the agent conflated it with Anthropic's Agent Skills (`SKILL.md`) format. **No origin attribution is made on that axis**; the only prior-art fact carried forward is the vault's own verified corpus record (v213). The same agent also confabulated that ToolJet's commits omit the model version (**899 of 905 trailer lines name one**) and that no `Claude-Session:` trailer exists (**11 do**) — three confabulations in one output, all about commits it never read from the clone.

**TWO NEW DECISION RULES (governance, not vocabulary).** **D26** — in a squash-merge repository a grep over commit **bodies** counts **pre-squash** commits, not commits: ToolJet squashes 35.6% of its history (6,087/17,101 subjects end `(#NNNN)`), and 165 commits carry 905 trailer lines (×5.48, max 143 in one); squashing also **hides commits entirely** — the agent layer's original `75eef168d` (2026-07-28) is not an ancestor of `main`. **D27** — declare the **ref population** of every git count and re-check it before accepting a refutation: `HEAD` and `--all` differ here by 8,512 commits (33%) and 56% on trailers, and **this ship's adversarial verifier returned REFUTED twice, on two unrelated claims, both times by silently switching to `--all`** while both claims were correct as stated. **D27 extends v241's D21** — a verifier can check the *right* claim against the *wrong* population and return a false refutation.

**Execution:** 16-agent `Workflow` (6 map surfaces → 8 adversarial verifiers → prior-art → completeness critic), **16/16 completed, 0 errors, ~1.69M subagent tokens, 430 tool uses, 778s** — `Workflow` **stable across five consecutive ships** (v239→v243). **12 errors caught, 4 of them the analyst's**, and 3 of those 4 caught by hand rather than by asking an agent.

**v244 OpenSandbox (2026-08-19) — ⭐ +1 §C standalone, ending a NINE-SHIP NO-MINT run (v235→v243):** **"Self-Hostable Sandbox / Code-Execution Runtime for AI Agents"** (**N=1**, CORPUS-FIRST for the surface, explicitly NOT world-first; anchor `opensandbox-group/OpenSandbox`, **Alibaba**, Apache-2.0). §28 ≤2-new-standalones cap honored (**1 of ≤2**). **§C live standalones 49 → 50; tracked surface ≈56 → ≈57. Counts 46/11 UNCHANGED.** Minted on two hand-verified grounds: **E2B appears 14 + 10 times across `_state/03c` + `_patterns/06` and every occurrence is as a dependency/substrate INSIDE another subject** (open-lovable v224 · Strix v190 · grok-build v215 · DSH v235 · ClawWork v233), with **no §C row describing the execution layer itself**; and it is **the last missing member of the agent-capability-layer family** (browser v41 · simulator v183 · web-reach v174 · Office v206 · stealth-browser v179 · database v212 · file-search v194 · video v198/v188 — each minted at N=1) ⇒ **capability-not-domain**, clearing the v196/v210/v211 bar. ⚠️ **NO-MINT alternative RECORDED and genuinely strong (flagged operator/audit-reviewable): `kubernetes-sigs/agent-sandbox` (K8s SIG, Nov 2025) standardised the shape 3 months before OpenSandbox's public launch and OpenSandbox SUPPORTS it (OSEP-0002 `implemented`) rather than setting it** — the v240/v242 decisive-external-prior-art discipline; plus OpenSandbox **delegates** isolation (`runtimeClassName` passthrough; no gVisor/Kata/Firecracker integration code in the tree) and **fame ≠ mint** (v222/v201). **LEANED MINT — the closest call since v233.**

**⭐⭐ RECORDED, NOT EXECUTED — FLAGGED TO AUDIT: the committed cross-harness agent-context bridge reaches N=3 and CROSSES the eligible-at-N=3 threshold (v203).** **v213 geti** (committed cross-harness symlinks, skill suite, N=1) → **v243 ToolJet** (`CLAUDE.md` = a 9-byte symlink to canonical `AGENTS.md`, 3 of 13 dirs; N=2, recorded+flagged by v243) → **v244 OpenSandbox** (`server/CLAUDE.md` = a 9-byte symlink, mode `120000`, **plus an 85-byte prose STUB at root**; N=3). Three independent, cross-author, cross-domain, **non-port** instances (Intel / ToolJet / Alibaba). ⚠️ **NOT self-promoted — a promotion is an audit act (the v232 rule, upheld at v235 and v243).** The audit must weigh: (i) the three are **not the same mechanism** ⇒ state the class **mechanism-agnostically** (*"a committed, drift-proof bridge from the harness-specific filename to a canonical `AGENTS.md`"*) or it fractures at N=3; (ii) **both multi-instance cases are PARTIAL** (ToolJet 3/13; OpenSandbox 2/6 **with two incompatible mechanisms in one repo, the stronger one in a subdirectory while the ROOT got the stub**) ⇒ the real-world form is *incomplete adoption*; (iii) the stub is drift-proof but **weaker for the harness** (costs a second read) ⇒ **rank** the mechanisms, do not merely enumerate them.

**Candidate axis RECORDED, NOT MINTED — the corpus's FIRST positive counter-example to the broken-authentication triad:** *"insecure-by-convenience mode behind a fail-closed informed-consent startup gate."* `server/opensandbox_server/startup_guard.py` takes the exact default that made **v231** (`require_auth:false` + wildcard CORS holding live sessions) and **v232** (auth fails open, `0.0.0.0`, wildcard CORS) **pilot-AVOID**, and gates it: an env-var path for CI, an **ANSI-red TTY prompt demanding the exact string `YES`**, a **30-second timeout that ABORTS**, a warning logged on every permissive start, a tracking-issue link, and a hard `RuntimeError` in non-interactive mode — documented in 4 places **plus two Chinese-language example configs**. **Not minted** (a mechanism within a security posture, one instance) but ~60 lines directly liftable. 🔴 One finding survives in the other direction: `middleware/auth.py:82-83` skips auth on `/v1/sandboxes/{id}/proxy/{port}/…` in single-tenant mode **even when an API key IS set** — the consent gate protects the operator who configured *nothing*, and a separate path un-protects the one who configured *something*. ⚠️ Whether `[ingress.secure_access]` covers it is **UNVERIFIED — flagged, not concluded.**

**Instance-strengthening, bookkeeping only (no N self-increment):** **#18 B1-MCP** +1 (`sdks/mcp/sandbox/python` → `pip install opensandbox-mcp`, stdio+http, documented for Claude Code + Cursor) · **#84 84c** +1 *and a notable new form* — the first instance from an **infrastructure vendor** rather than a skills project, and the first to distinguish **copy** targets (a skills *directory*: Claude Code / Cursor / Codex) from **append** targets (a single instruction *file*: Copilot / Windsurf / Cline), rendering all six from one canonical source via `render_skill_for_target()` · **#83** ×2 · **#66 positive exemplar** joining **pi v228** + **ToolJet v243** (19 lockfiles, **zero npm lifecycle scripts** across all 5 `package.json`, no lockfile mirror substitution, cosign keyless signing + provenance attestations, OpenSSF Best Practices **passing 100%**) · **#19** (a) FAIL corporate-not-Anthropic (§41, the NVIDIA v169 precedent).

**THREE NEW DECISION RULES (governance, not vocabulary).** **D28** — **an absent config file is not an absent check**: all 24 workflows grep ZERO for `codeql|trivy|gosec|bandit|semgrep|dependency-review|scorecard|snyk` and there is no `.github/dependabot.yml`, **yet 18 `Copilot Autofix` commits and 5 `dependabot/` branches prove both run via GitHub SETTINGS, invisible in the tree** ⇒ a tree-only audit systematically **UNDER-reports** automated security posture, and **the detector is the bot's commit trailers, not the workflow directory**. Completes the trio with D26 (what a body-grep counts) and D27 (which refs it counts over). **D29** — **`mtime` is not drift; test the claims, not the date**: a critic called `cli/AGENTS.md` "67 days stale"; all 11 paths it names EXIST ⇒ **REFUTED**. Staleness is *content disagreeing with reality*. ⭐ **This is the rule that decides whether `verify-vault-docs` is a linter or a recency alarm.** **D30** — **when the API is mocked, read the repo's own committed badges**: a hardcoded `Stars-11.6k` badge, replaced in a dated commit (`e435df40`, 2026-07-06), yielded a **dated** metric that §37.4 otherwise forbids. **D26 EXTENDED** — the commits↔trailer-lines gap **measures merge strategy**: v243 = 165 commits / 905 lines (mean **5.48**, squash-dominant) vs v244 = 197 / 340 (mean **1.73**, merge-commit-dominant, 726 merge commits) ⇒ same metric, different meaning; report the ratio *and* the strategy.

**FLAGGED TO AUDIT, NOT SELF-EXECUTED:**
- 🔴 **Pattern #45 Dual-Licensing — CONFIRMED (promoted v60, N=2 with AutoGPT v59), but its v23-anchored sub-variant 45a is STALE AS WRITTEN.** 45a reads *"Apache core + AGPL UI"*; at HEAD the AGPL side is **76% of the tree** (3,162 AGPL-tagged files) and **inside the wheel's default package list** (`pyproject.toml:79-81` includes `unsloth_cli*` — 36/36 AGPL — and `studio.backend*`), while `pyproject.toml:11` declares `license = "Apache-2.0"`. ⭐ **The irony is exact: the vault celebrates #45's promotion as a "35-wiki stale-then-un-stale corpus-record" PROVING stale-tracking works — and the anchor that validated the machinery has itself gone stale.** Needs a REWRITE, not an N-bump.

**The §C standalone candidate — *"Sub-100M On-Device Tool-Calling Foundation Model Shipped as an Agent-Callable Library with Constrained Decoding"* — is DECLINED on five independent grounds, three of them this registry's own written discipline:**

1. **The MODEL-TIER RULE binds, and this is its 5th data point.** §F v176 GLM-5 verbatim: *"**NO §C standalone minted** — a single goal-adjacent frontier model is not a recurring capability class; **§C vocab is tool/capability-shaped**"*; §F v193 TimesFM **DECLINED** a *"corpus-first time-series-forecasting FM"* mint on identical reasoning despite being the corpus's first forecasting FM. ⇒ **the descriptive Model/Inference-Substrate tier becomes N=5** — GLM-5 v176 · DeepSpec v186 · TimesFM v193 · PixelRAG v211 · **needle v246** (**bookkeeping only; counts unchanged**).
2. **NOT world-first, in a crowded band.** FunctionGemma 270M · LFM2 / LFM2.5 · Apple FM on-device · Octopus v2 · the Gorilla / ToolLLM / xLAM / Hammer line — the README names three of them as peers. Grammar-constrained decoding is **standard practice** (llama.cpp GBNF, Outlines, XGrammar, Guidance, JSON mode). **What is distinctive is SCALE — and scale is a number, not a class** (~5–6× below the smallest widely-cited peer) ⇒ the **PixelRAG v211** discipline: *corpus-first-for-a-technique ≠ a mintable §C class.* ⚠️ **An agent's "WORLD-FIRST at 45M" verdict was REJECTED** — an absence-of-evidence argument, **falsified inside the same product line** (the org's own `cactus` repo documents Needle at **26m**), and it confuses a number with a class.
3. **domain-not-capability** — phones / wearables / smart home / robots (the **meetily v196** + **AIRI v210** discipline).
4. **§28 anti-inflation** — no cluster to join, no N=2 partner in the corpus.
5. ⭐ **The distinctive machinery is NOT IN THE SUBJECT.** `needle/__init__.py:38-48` binds exactly **four** C symbols, so the **learned tool-retrieval head** (the "top-5" is not in this repo), the **byte-level grammar** and inference-time **confidence** all live in a binary fetched at runtime from a Hugging Face model repo. Minting here would mean minting a class on **prose describing unread code** ⇒ **v242's D25 applied to a COMPONENT rather than a subject.** There is **no benchmark data, no eval harness and no reproduction script** anywhere in the repo.

**Subject.** "ego lite", a Chromium-based **macOS browser built so a human and any number of external AI agents share ONE browser** (per-agent isolated **Task Spaces** inheriting the user's login state), plus **`ego-browser`**, the open connection layer exposing the browser to any agent CLI as an in-page JavaScript tool surface. **MIT repo; the browser itself is a closed-source `.dmg`.** Page-stated **11.9k★ / 624 forks** (§37.4 — mocked API, **NOT #52**). Org **CitroLabs**; dominant committer pseudonymous ("Tachikoma" / `section9lab@`). ✅ **SOURCE-CLONED TWICE** (HEAD `c46a439e`, 2026-08-10; **150 tracked files / ~26,800 lines**; **245 commits `HEAD` / 415 `--all`**, 70 refs, 21 tags, **2 roots** — `f4ae5dcf` is HEAD's ancestor, `144b0734` is an orphan). **GOAL-ALIGNED INCLUDE 3/4** [(a) FAIL §41 · (b) **STRONG** — cleanly on the goal-#1 agent-capability substrate, no §40 · (c) STRONG ⚠️ tempered · (d) STRONG]. **COLLISION CLEAN.** **Pattern #57: NONE.**

**MINT — NO, on four independent grounds** (the collision agent and the critic concurred independently):
1. **Form-factor-within-a-genre.** A browser is a product genre — ruled twice already: **lobehub v222** (*fame is not a mint*; world-canonical ≠ world-first) and **hermes-webui v227**.
2. **A combination is not a capability class.** All three components are occupied by strong prior art: *daily-use browser whose agent reaches your logins* → **Claude in Chrome / ChatGPT Atlas / Perplexity Comet**; *isolated parallel workspaces in one browser* → **Arc Spaces** (the name is literally Arc's) / **Firefox Multi-Account Containers** / **Chrome profiles** / the whole **anti-detect** category (**CloakBrowser v69**, **camofox v179**); *external-agent-drivable browser* → **CDP/Playwright / Playwright MCP / Chrome DevTools MCP / browser-use v41 / Vercel agent-browser**. §C vocabulary is tool/capability-shaped. ⚠️ **Arc and Brave already ship first-class agent APIs, Arc's with a declarative JSON permission system** — a control this subject lacks.
3. **The distinctive machinery is NOT IN THE SUBJECT** — the *"kernel-level customization / strongest Snapshot / deeply nested iframes"* differentiator lives in the closed binary (`driver/observe.ts` ≈139 lines calling `browserEgo().snapshot()`). The same ground on which **v246 needle** declined.
4. **§28** anti-re-accumulation + the N=1 gate.

⭐ **RECORDED NOT EXECUTED — the losing alternative (DEFERRED watch axis, N=1):** **"Human+Agent Shared-State Co-Habitation with Per-Workspace Ownership Handoff."** The genuinely distinctive artifact is not the browser but **the per-Space ownership state machine** — `agent | agentDelegatedToUser | user`, with `handOff` / `takeOver` / `claim` / `waitForAgentControl` as first-class operations, a tabulated per-helper policy for user-owned Spaces, and a doctrine naming deference as success. Repeatable beyond browsers (an IDE, a terminal multiplexer, a shared design canvas) ⇒ a real N=2 candidate if a second implementation ships. ⚠️ Not minted: **an absence of evidence is not evidence of firstness** (the argument a v246 agent got wrong and had rejected), and **a promotion is an audit act** (the v232 rule).

**Instance-strengthening — recorded, NOT self-incremented (N-tallies are audit bookkeeping):**

**⭐ TWO NEW RULES (the ship's transferable output):**
- **D34 — a committed CI rule is not an enforced one.** (**v244 D28 in reverse:** D28 said an absent config file is not an absent check; the converse holds.) Anchor: `main-pr-source.yml` fails any PR into `main` not sourced from `dev`; added 2026-06-01; **since then 82 PR merges into `main`, 16 from `dev`, 66 from branches the gate rejects — including HEAD**, while `CONTRIBUTING.md:283` tells contributors to *"branch from latest `main`"*, `:322` says merge via `dev`, the pre-commit hook gates on `origin/dev`, and `main`/`dev` have **diverged** (32 ahead / 20 behind). ⚠️ Whether the gate is a *required* status check lives in branch-protection settings, **not in the tree** — so the claim is a measurement, never an accusation. ⭐ **DETECTOR (portable, one command — this ship's method contribution alongside v246's `grep -rni "silent" .`): take a gate's own predicate and test it against the history it was supposed to govern.** *A gate you have never seen fail is not a gate you have seen pass.*
- **D35 — prose goes stale in proportion to how little MACHINERY and how few READERS touch it, not by whether its audience is a human or a machine.** (**Refines D22**, which predicted that *agent-facing* prose goes stale first.) Anchor: a **within-repo controlled comparison** — same repo, same authors, same 73 days. The shipped `SKILL.md` (209 lines, read on every agent run, rewritten by CI, published to two registries) is **clean**; the developer-facing docs have **five verified defects**: a link to a `SKILL.zh.md` deleted at `1a7f293`; the Four Principles attributed to an `AGENTS.md` that contains none of them; `@eN` taught in four places when `AGENTS.md:17` explicitly warns *"`@21`, **not** `@e21`"*; `AGENTS.md` naming a `run-e2e.sh` that no longer exists; and `AGENTS.md` describing `npm run e2e` as a suite `package.json` does not map it to. ⚠️ **Vault application is exact:** `CLAUDE.md` is high-contact and maintained; the `_state/03c-**v183**` filename label and the shim↔`_patterns/06` divergence are the lowest-contact surfaces — and **the v246 E3 error (a stale shim premise fed to 14 agents) is this same mechanism.**

**⭐ AND THE HEADLINE, for the record:** the repository **declares the closed-source boundary unprompted** — `README.md` (License) *"The ego lite browser is a separate, free download"* and `AGENTS.md:3-5` *"provided by the **closed-source** ego lite app … **not** the browser itself."* ⇒ **against v244 → v245 → v246 this CLOSES A SET OF FOUR** in which the licence in prose did not describe the artifact that runs (over-assert → under-assert → split-and-silent → **split-and-declared**). It is the counter-example the set needed, and it costs one sentence. ⚠️ **The other omission tested and DROPPED:** the unmentioned paid `ego` / Business tier is **not** a v246-shaped omission — `lite.ego.app/enterprise` prices the free tier *"$0 forever"* and sells **support/SLA/onboarding, not withheld features**; nothing about the user's rights or software is affected, so the README's *"Free ✓"* row is defensible.

**⭐ THE THESIS (why the ship is worth its space): this project writes excellent checks and does not connect them.** Its own doctrine is *"write the check first"* (`CONTRIBUTING.md` §12, Goal-Driven Execution). It then hand-rolls **real mutation testing** on `acorn` aimed exactly at the predicates whose silent failure does damage (`isAgentOwned`, `findMatchingTaskSpace`, `boxModelCenter`) — **unwired**; writes **a style linter for the prose that instructs an LLM** (prose-vs-**doctrine**, not v240's prose-vs-code) — **unwired**; and builds a skill-publish pipeline (version normalisation, **a token-leak scanner over the release archive**, a SHA256-pinned CLI, dry-run, post-publish verify) that **triggers on a tag pattern no tag in the repo matches — it has never fired**. The one check wired in all four places validates the site-learning **data format**. ⭐ **Conversely it gets right the thing v246 got wrong:** its real-browser suite (24 modules / 31 cases) cannot run on `ubuntu-latest`, so instead of letting it **skip** in the release gate (v246: *pytest reports a skip as success*) it moved it into the **pre-commit hook, where the closed browser exists**, and CI claims only what it ran ⇒ **PORTABLE RULE: put the test where the dependency is, and let the gate claim only what it ran.** ⚠️ Bypassable with `--no-verify`, opt-in, and undisclosed in `CONTRIBUTING.md` = a silent contract.

🔴 **PILOT: DO NOT INSTALL.** *The only configuration in which it is safe for this operator is the one that deletes the reason to use it* — its feature is inheriting real logins; decline that and it is a closed binary from an unidentified team driving pages that don't matter, which Playwright does in the open on three platforms. Candidate sourcing fails on the **RATIFIED ADR** (no injection defence, no read-only mode, no approval gate ⇒ fails all five clauses), on **asymmetric account risk**, and on **unestablished accountability**. ✅ **BORROW: M1** the write probe (*on failure change TECHNIQUE, not parameters*) · **M2** D34's detector against the vault's own gates · **M3** deference-as-success · **M4** the `help()` pattern (generate the reference from the code) · **M5** the injection paragraph they never wrote, into the candidate-LLM ADR.

**Workflow:** 14 agents (5 map → 5 pipelined **contradiction** → 3 situate → 1 critic); **11/12 completed, 1 errored, 3 empty-result, ~1.53M subagent tokens, 393 tool uses, 817 s.** ⚠️ 3 contradiction passes + the prior-art agent failed schema ⇒ prior art researched **by hand**; mapper outputs **recovered from `journal.jsonl`**. ⭐⭐ **METHOD — v246's finding REPLICATES and the fix WORKS:** the verify stage was rebuilt as a **contradiction** stage (mapper report handed over as a *suspect document*; job = produce corrections by independent reading; ≥6 citations opened and confirmed; a **read receipt** naming files and line ranges required) and it returned corrections and receipts instead of confirmations. ⇒ **Do not ask an agent whether a claim is true; ask it what the claim gets wrong, and make it show you what it read.** **ERROR LEDGER 9 caught, 5 MINE (all self-caught pre-publication; three were suspicions that did NOT survive checking), 4 the fleet's** — incl. ⭐ the critic "correcting" 148 files to **173** by counting `.git/` internals (**the vault's signature error shape committed by the agent assigned to hunt it** ⇒ *adjudicate a counting dispute with a command, not with authority*) and ⭐ the critic calling the binary **`libneedle.*`** — **v246's** binary, imported from the vault context I supplied ⇒ **rejected**, and *the lesson is mine: context supplied to ground an agent can also contaminate it.*

An LLM-driven generator of ARC-AGI-1-style puzzles, distribution-matched to the public eval set, published as the artifact of **BDH-CQ** (arXiv:2608.09888).

- **MINT DECLINED on five grounds.** **(1) Decisive external prior art — `google/ARC-GEN`** (Apache-2.0, arXiv:2511.00162) is an explicitly **"mimetic"** ARC generator preserving grid size, colour distribution and pair counts, i.e. *distribution-matched ARC task generation already published* — **and it is named in BDH-CQ's own §4.2 training mixture**; joined by **BARC/ARC-Heavy** (LLM-generated, ~400k, 2024), **RE-ARC** (400 procedural generators, arXiv:2404.07353), **ARC-TGI** (461 generators incl. 15 ARC-AGI-1 test tasks), and the contamination-resistant-eval practice generally (LiveBench / Dynabench / GSM1k / canary strings / ARC Prize's own semi-private + private splits) ⇒ the **v240** (awesome-selfhosted data repo + HACS) / **v242** (Eclipse RCP/OSGi) discipline. **(2) Technique-not-capability** — the **v211 PixelRAG** rule. **(3) Research artifact, not a capability layer** — 2,139 lines of paper appendix with **no agent-facing surface whatsoever** (no MCP, no skill, no `AGENTS.md`/`CLAUDE.md`/`llms.txt`); contrast every mint-at-N=1 capability precedent (v183 serve-sim / v194 fff / v206 OfficeCLI / v215 grok-build / v244 OpenSandbox), each of which an agent reaches *through*. **(4) §28** anti-inflation at 51 live standalones. **(5) Not the exemplar** — ARC-GEN is Google's and larger.
- ⚠️ **NOT an N=2 of the v233 row "Economic-Survival Agent Benchmark."** That row's object is a **benchmark HARNESS** (agents solve real professional tasks, are paid by rubric, pay their own inference, go bankrupt). This is a **dataset GENERATOR** emitting `tasks.json` and explicitly declining to score (`generate_tasks.py:22-24`: *"Scoring a model against the generated set is deliberately out of scope"*). Different object ⇒ **no N-bump**, and the corpus's eval/benchmark family remains **N=1**.
- ⚠️ **NO-MINT ALTERNATIVE RECORDED — operator/audit-reviewable, NOT self-executed:** *"LLM-Generated Distribution-Matched Private Benchmark Twin for Contamination Control"* (N=1), defensible only on the narrow conjunction of **joint-slot covariance sampling** (all slot properties read off ONE anchor task so natural covariance survives, `:140-152`) + a **code-enforced eval-content firewall** (`:641-643`, *"only ever removes; it never instructs"*). Loses on all five grounds; minting it would be **drawing the circle to fit the subject inside** (camofox v179).
- ⭐ **NEW DEFERRED WATCH AXIS (recorded, NOT minted): "benchmark-contamination tooling — generate-a-private-twin as an evaluation-integrity practice."** N=1. The corpus's **first subject whose object is the trustworthiness of a NUMBER** rather than a capability. Eligible at a genuine N=2; a 2nd instance must be a tool for *establishing that a measurement is uncontaminated*, not another benchmark.
- **PATTERN LAYER (tracked at their own layers, NOT here, NOT minted):** **#83 STRONG** — a named Caveats section disclosing that the tool's central quality guarantee is unverified (*"Solvability is not verified programmatically … 19 [of] 20 were solvable"*), plus ⭐ **a PRE-REGISTERED INSTRUMENT-INVALIDITY GUARD** (`label_eval_tasks.py:211-216`: *"'other' is the escape hatch; a large share means the closed list is wrong … and should not be analysed as-is"*, emitted as a runtime WARNING above **15%**), plus two dead-flag messages naming the defects that once disabled the filters. **#84 84c** provider-agnostic via `OPENAI_BASE_URL`/`ARCGEN_MODEL` (**no N-bump**). **#66 LOW** — no port, no CORS, no `exec` on model output, two egress hosts, key from env only, writes confined to `data/`; **no broken-auth triad structurally** (there is no server); zero tests. **#19 19a** first Pathway / Arnould subject. **#57 NONE.** **#18 B1-MCP NO.** **#12 NO.** **#52 CANNOT CLAIM** (§37.4 — API mocked; 3.6k★/21 forks/**1 watcher** page-stated). Max **#85**.
- ⭐⭐ **CROSS-SHIP METHOD CONTRIBUTIONS.** **v246's `grep -rni "silent"` replicates — 4th repo — with a refinement:** exactly 2 hits, and **both are places the author NOTICED a silence and removed it** (`:523` a documented trade-off immediately followed by the real termination condition; `generate_tasks_stratified.py:232` *"silently excluded"* followed by a print of how many) ⇒ v246 found engineered loud failures, v248 an evidence ladder, **v249 a candid docstring** — the detector keeps finding where the author thought about silent wrongness, and the *shape* of what it finds characterises the codebase. **v247/v248's code-gate-vs-prose axis: this subject carries BOTH SIDES IN ONE 905-LINE FILE** — the guarantee protecting the *experiment* is code-enforced (the firewall, structural validation with regeneration, slot pinning), the guarantee making each task a valid *puzzle* is prose addressed to `gpt-5.6` (*"unambiguously inferable"*, *"verify the task yourself"*), and **nothing checks that a generated task's grid sizes match the slot it was given** ⇒ the sharpest available statement of the axis. **v240's INVENTORY RULE, one instance:** `load_anchor_profiles()` reports **how many** unlabelled anchors were dropped from the pool, never **which**.

A Claude Code / Codex / **Pi** / Cowork **agent skill** rendering **28 visual types** as self-contained HTML+SVG under an editorial design system, plus URL→brand-token onboarding and draw.io/Mermaid redraw. MIT. Author **Cathryn Lavery** (BestSelf Co founder, littlemight.com, **trained architect**) — a disclosed individual, **not Anthropic**; notability is explicitly not an (a) signal.

**Grounds for NO MINT (five):**

1. **A clean instance of CONFIRMED Pattern #88 "Anti-Slop-Curation" at sub-typology 88c "machinery-with-enforcement."** The tagline is literally the pattern's vocabulary — *"No shadows, no **Mermaid-slop**"* — and the README states the rationale in its idiom (the 4px grid is *"non-negotiable, it's what keeps the diagrams from feeling AI-generated"*). Joins **impeccable v75 / taste-skill v81 / huashu-design v82 / open-design v83 / ui-ux-pro-max v85 / hallmark v204 / ui-skills v218**; **hallmark v204 is the direct precedent** (a strong design skill by a notable org → 88c instance, no mint). N-tally is audit bookkeeping, **not self-incremented**.
2. **Decisive FIRST-PARTY prior art.** Anthropic ships an **`artifact-diagramming`** skill for agents (*"…the inline-SVG mechanics that keep it legible in both themes"* — present in the analysing session's own skill roster) and Artifacts **render Mermaid natively**. The guidance surface is occupied by the platform.
3. **Not world-first** for "declarative source → opinionated diagram": Mermaid, D2, PlantUML, Graphviz, `mingrammer/diagrams`, Structurizr, draw.io, Excalidraw, Kroki, Eraser DiagramGPT, Napkin.ai. The **v222 lobehub** rule — *world-canonical is not world-first; fame is not a mint.*
4. **Form-factor-within-a-genre** — delivering an established genre as an agent skill is packaging, not a capability class (**v227 hermes-webui / v236 dsh-TUI**).
5. **§28** at 51 live standalones, and it is not the exemplar of the class it would open.

⚠️ **STRONGEST NO-MINT ALTERNATIVE — RECORDED, reviewable, NOT self-executed:** a §C standalone at **N=1**, *"Aesthetic/Editorial Design Ruleset with Machine-Verified Output Geometry."* **Genuinely strong on mechanism** — nothing in the 7-member design-skill cluster compiles its rules into executable geometric assertions; every prior instance is a checklist, forbidden-list or critique rubric, whereas this one numerically verifies that a treemap's area encoding does not lie about its own printed values and tests its verifiers adversarially in both directions. **Declined on two grounds:** the mechanism is a **TECHNIQUE, not a capability class** (the **v211 PixelRAG** discipline), and the verified-geometry machinery **DOES NOT SHIP TO THE USER** — `verify-geometry.py`, `verify-treemap.py` and `lint-skin.py` stay in the repo; the installed footprint is 3 files / 2,603 lines whose only output checker is `self_check.py` (389 lines: accessibility contract, single-file safety, motion) ⇒ **v242 D25 / v246: the machinery is not in the artifact you get.** Flagged to the overdue audit; **a mint is an audit act (v232).**

**Corpus contributions (recorded, no counts moved):**

- ⭐⭐⭐ **The v246→v250 code-vs-prose set reaches FIVE, and this is the first member to ARTICULATE the boundary rather than merely fall on one side.** `SKILL.md:261` calls six connector rules *"non-negotiable"*; **exactly one has a gate**, and `verify-geometry.py:9-14` states the criterion: *"**Paint order is what makes this a defect rather than a stylistic choice**."* ⇒ **THE RULE: you can only compile the part of your aesthetic that becomes a LIE when violated; the rest stays prose — and when you leave one in prose, do not call it non-negotiable.**
- ⭐⭐⭐ **The v240 INVENTORY RULE gets its controlled experiment** — inside one 236-line file: `verify-docs-sync.py` → `check_gallery` (:91-103) runs **both** set-differences (correct); `check_readme_tree` (:116-125), forty lines later, runs **only** tree→disk. Measured consequence: the README tree names **14 of 28** `type-*.md` and **7 of 28** `scripts/*.py`, **CI green**. Same file, same author ⇒ **not a competence gap; an AIM gap.**
- ⭐⭐ **v247 D34 tested and PASSED — the first clean pass in the run.** 26 of 28 scripts wired in `ci.yml`, **0 unwired gates**, **0 `continue-on-error`**, **0 `|| true`**, 25 × `if: always()` (the *correct* implementation: all gates report, every failure still fatal), 3-OS × 2-Python matrix + a Python 3.9 compat job.

- ⭐⭐ **v245 D32 found independently** — `docs/adr/0002:15`: *"27 when this record was accepted; **see Amendments for the current figure**."* Its Amendments section also contributes the run's best sentence: *"a PR that edits them without amending this file **has quietly made itself the authority**… **the number in the test is just whatever the last contributor typed**."*

Best two: `verify-slopegraph.py:170-176` (*"`float("nan")` succeeds and then every `abs(x) > tolerance` comparison is False, so a NaN coordinate silently satisfied every check in the file"* — a general bug class for **any** threshold-based verifier) and `:323` (*"**dropping it silently is how a lie ships**"* — the *not-in-scope* vs *in-scope-but-unreadable* distinction, **exactly the rule that would have prevented v240's dropped submission**).

**§F — v252 context-os (`jacob-dietle/context-os`) — 2026-08-20 — NO MINT; counts 46/11 UNCHANGED; §C live standalones 51 unchanged; tracked surface ≈58 unchanged.** GOAL-ALIGNED INCLUDE 3/4 [(a) FAIL §41 · (b) **STRONG** · (c) STRONG ⚠️tempered · (d) STRONG].

**SUBJECT.** An MIT agent-skill collection (13 skills, 8,243 md lines) + templates that scaffolds a Karpathy-style LLM wiki — atomic notes, `[[wiki-links]]`, `emergent → validated → canonical`, a `SENSE → ORIENT → ACT → DEPOSIT` loop — behind a **closed-source `context-os` CLI** supplying graph queries, **"file heat"** and behavioural **"co-access"**. Source-verified by two clones (`diff -rq` clean both ways), HEAD `1027e3f1`, 26 commits, one root, one author, 108★ page-stated.

**MINT DECLINED on FIVE grounds.**
1. **⭐ DECISIVE — direct in-corpus precedent at v234** (`agentic-local-brain`, **three days prior**): a near-identical subject — a personal knowledge system whose headline feature was an auto-compiled LLM wiki — was declined **NO MINT on four grounds**. Declining v234 and minting v252 would be incoherent.
2. **NOT corpus-first for the domain.** **v134** (`obsidian-second-brain`, explicitly *"an evolution of Karpathy's LLM Wiki pattern"*) and **v118** (OpenHuman Memory Tree) precede; v234 precedes by three days. This is the **5th instance** on the already-registered **Pattern #57 sub-variant** *"Productized/Automated Karpathy-LLM-Wiki-Pattern at the Methodology-Influence Layer."*
3. **🔴 THE MACHINERY DOES NOT SHIP (v242 D25 / v246).** The only mechanically novel element — **file heat + behavioural co-access driving which knowledge survives** — lives **entirely in the closed binary**, which the README itself calls *"not fully finished yet."* **A capability class cannot be minted on a mechanism absent from the artifact.** Sufficient alone.
4. **Technique/domain-not-capability** (v211 PixelRAG / v196 meetily / v234 discipline). *"Instrument your knowledge base and delete what nobody reads"* is a technique; PKM is a domain. Neither is §C-vocab-shaped.
5. **§28** at 51 live standalones, and it is **not the exemplar** of its class — 26 commits, one author, **108★** against a genre with 34k★/63k★ incumbents (the v180 / v234 weak-anchor precedent).

⚠️ **STRONGEST ALTERNATIVE RECORDED, operator/audit-reviewable, NOT self-executed:** a §C standalone at N=1 — ***"Stigmergically-Instrumented Knowledge Base — an agent-maintained graph whose retention is driven by measured access rather than declared taxonomy."*** Genuinely distinctive on mechanism: **every prior instance on the #57 vector BUILDS the wiki; this is the first to MEASURE whether it is being read and DELETE what is not.** v118/v134/v137/v234 all accumulate; this one prunes on evidence, and its own v1→v2 refactor is that idea applied to itself. **Loses** on grounds 3–5 — decisively **3** — and would be *drawing the circle to make it first* (the camofox v179 discipline).
