### §F — v254 awesome-cli-coding-agents (2026-08-20) — NO MINT

**Subject:** `bradAGI/awesome-cli-coding-agents` — a **300-entry** curated index of CLI coding agents + harnesses in ONE Markdown file, plus a 192-line Python script and a **weekly GitHub Actions job** that re-fetches every star count from the GitHub API and re-sorts each section. Two clones, `diff -rq` clean both ways; HEAD `6e307e73`; 125 commits, ONE root (2026-02-06), 4 files ever tracked, **NO LICENCE EVER**, 37 identities (Brad 72 across THREE — D26/D27), bus factor one. Page-stated ⇒ **NOT #52**.

**GOAL-ALIGNED INCLUDE 3/4** [(a) FAIL §41 — bare handle `bradAGI`, **0 hits in vault state**, no declared Anthropic affiliation · (b) **STRONG** — the canonical index of the goal-#1 category, 39.7% of entries naming Claude Code · (c) STRONG · (d) STRONG]. No §40 needed.

**NO MINT on five grounds — instance #10 of CONFIRMED Pattern #68 "Awesome-List-Genre Meta-Pattern"** (promoted at the **v31** mini-audit as the corpus's first meta-pattern-at-N=3: build-your-own-x v8 / awesome-design-md v25 / awesome-mcp-servers v31; refined v50; later v170, v201, v218, v240, v253): (1) #68 covers it, nine prior instances; (2) **domain-not-capability** (v196/v193/v210); (3) **technique-not-capability (v211)** — the distinctive part is an editorial mechanism; (4) **not world-first and precedence NOT ESTABLISHED** (automated star refresh is a known practice; any first-mover claim would rest on `created_at` from the **mocked** API ⇒ **v222**); (5) **§28** at 51 standalones against a bus-factor-one anchor.

**⚠️ STRONGEST ALTERNATIVE — recorded, reviewable, NOT self-executed:** a **§C standalone at N=1, *"Curated Directory Whose Ordering Invariant Is Machine-Enforced While Its Membership Invariants Are Not."*** Genuinely distinctive on mechanism — **of the nine prior #68 instances NONE pairs a running weekly CI job with an entirely unguarded uniqueness/liveness invariant, and the pairing is what produces the finding.** **Decisively declined** because, besides grounds 2–5, **v253 has no machinery at all and therefore cannot serve as the N=2 of a machinery-based class** — the alternative would stand alone at N=1.

**⭐⭐⭐ THE HEADLINE — the machinery fetches the answer to every question the list gets wrong, parses it into memory, and reads one field.** `fetch_stars()` line 49 is `return json.load(resp).get("stargazers_count")`: the whole `GET /repos/{owner}/{repo}` response is parsed and one key selected. The same object carries **`archived`** (🔴 `letta-ai/lettabot`, 327★, sits in the ACTIVE list — I fetched it, GitHub title reads *"Archived - has been replaced by Letta Code"*), **`full_name`** (🔴 `ruvnet/claude-flow`, 68,000★, **redirects to `ruvnet/ruflo`**; also `paoloanzn/free-code`→`freecodexyz`), **`pushed_at`** (🔴 no decay policy exists at all) and **`license`** (🔴 134 of 300 entries silent). ⭐⭐⭐ **And v253, with ZERO machinery, caught BOTH** — its hand-maintained `Resting` section retired lettabot with dated evidence, and its 2026-07-29 pass verified the `claude-flow`→`ruflo` rebrand **on the very day v254's maintainer did his own rebrand pass, fixed a different one, and missed this.** Meanwhile the script builds `repos`, a dict keyed by `(owner, repo)` — **building it IS deduplication** — and prints `len(repos)`: **every Monday for 17 weeks it has printed 295 while editing a file with 300 entry lines**, and **5 byte-identical adjacent duplicates** (all from one commit, `126e206` 2026-07-29, surviving FOUR machine rewrites) have never been detected.

**⭐⭐⭐ THE RULE, extending v250 — A DECLARATION IS A FORM OF AIM.** Three classes of fact, three fates: what the **machine** was aimed at (stars, sort order, date stamp) is **correct and verifiable** (zero sort violations in 300 entries; I verified three star counts against rendered pages — `openclaw` 386,827 vs `387k` exact, `claude-code` 142,065 vs `142k` exact, `hermes-agent` 233,195 vs `232k` at 0.4% drift over 3 days); what the **human declared** (*"110+"* = **exactly 111 agents**, 83+12+16, harnesses being *"plus"*) is **correct, because he bumped it by hand five separate times**; what **nobody declared and nothing was aimed at** (uniqueness, liveness, rename-freshness) is **broken**. ⇒ **Before adding a check, ask what you have never declared. That is where the rot is** — and the vault's own case is exact: the chapter filename was never declared to mean anything, so it has been wrong for **71 ships**.

**⭐⭐⭐ THE THREE-SHIP DECIDABILITY SYNTHESIS IS NOW COMPLETE.** v250: *you can only compile the part of your aesthetic that becomes a **lie** when violated* (and don't call unguarded rules non-negotiable). v253: *compile the merely **stylistic** part and the machine is wrong while the human is right* (21 lint deviations, wrong 21 times). **v254: they BUILT the machine and AIMED it at the one decidable thing nobody doubted, leaving both decidable-and-actually-broken things unchecked** — `awesome-lint` would fail this file ~13 times and be **RIGHT 6** (5 duplicates + the missing `LICENSE`) and **WRONG 7** (6 lowercase starts, **every one a proper noun**: `xAI`, `tmux`, `macOS`×4, plus one backticked `pip install`). ⭐⭐ **And v253's false-positive class REPLICATES: it found 4, all `tmux`/`macOS`. Two lists, two authors, TEN instances, TEN proper nouns ⇒ an independent N=2 that the capitalization rule is SYSTEMATICALLY defective on a technology catalogue.**

**SECONDARY:** ⭐⭐⭐ **#57 — a NEW WIDEST FAN-IN RECORD, 16 prior numbered ships as entries** (v9 deer-flow · v15 multica · v30 OpenHands · v36/v228 pi-mono · v42 claude-flow · v48 gh-aw · v52 oh-my-openagent · v72 CodeWhale · v99 cmux · v144 headroom · v150 paseo · v162 agent-of-empires · v177 kilocode · v179 camofox-browser · v195 openwiki · v215 grok-build), **spanning 206 versions; prior record v253's 10** — recorded, NOT self-incremented. ⭐⭐ **#57 in the other direction: the vault ALREADY CITED this list twice, at v223, as evidence that "harness-engineering is a recognized 2026 field" — a prior ship used it as a source, this ship makes it the subject.** ⭐⭐ **Pattern #18 B1-MCP (≈N=13) gets ECOSYSTEM-SCALE evidence rather than another instance: across 300 CLI-agent tools MCP is the single most-named mechanism (53, 17.7%), ahead of skills (45) and worktrees (26)** — and this **CORRECTS v253's strongest generalisation**, which measured worktree at 16.1% and called it *"the load-bearing primitive of the whole category"* with MCP at 3.3%. **Not a contradiction, a scoping correction: parallelism is solved with git worktrees; capability is solved with MCP and skills.** ⭐ **#83 POSITIVE ×11** — eleven restrictive-licence disclosures incl. `tlbx` (101★) AGPL-3.0 and **`TeDDy` (4★) AGPL**; **licence disclosure 166 of 300 = 55.3% vs v253's 4 of 180 = 2.2%, a 25× improvement with the adverse selection GONE** and a **U-shaped** rate (smallest projects disclose most); independent cross-list confirmation — `loki-mode` discloses BUSL-1.1 in **both** lists. **NOT #52.** **#12** — no `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, **no agent-facing surface at all.** **#66** N/A for the artifact; the risk is the 282 repos it points at.

**⭐ NEW DEFERRED WATCH AXIS (N=1):** *"the partially-automated directory — a curated list with a live CI job whose aim is narrower than the list's own stated criteria."*

**⚠️⚠️ v246 `silent` DETECTOR: ZERO — second consecutive null, and I RAN it** (v252's worst self-error was recording this null without running it). ⭐⭐⭐ **But v253's explanation no longer holds and the failure sharpens the rule:** v253 reasoned *"a curated list has no runtime… the detector's domain is artifacts that RUN."* **v254 HAS a runtime — 192 lines running unattended every Monday — and still returns zero** ⇒ **the detector's domain is not artifacts that run but ARTIFACTS WHOSE AUTHORS HAVE DEBUGGED THEM.** And this runtime fails silently exactly where it matters: an `HTTPError` prints one `WARN` to **stderr in an unattended weekly CI log**, then `key()` falls back to re-parsing **the badge already printed in the file**, so a dead repo keeps its last-known star count forever as current fact; and if every fetch failed, `git diff --quiet` would find no change, print `"No changes."` and **exit 0** — a green, silent, successful run. ⭐ **A warning in an unattended job is a silent failure with extra steps.**

**`inflation_check` HELD** — 0 mints; the §C alternative recorded and declined per §28 + v222 + v211; counts **46/11** unchanged; §C live standalones **51** unchanged; surface **≈58** unchanged; max pattern #85; **no N-bumps taken** (the #57 fan-in record and the #83 ×11 are recorded for the audit, not self-incremented).

**Streak: v253 `GA:111` → `GA:112 · OG:13 [7 ov]`** (**35 consecutive GA**, v220→v254); **§35 CLEAR** (window {v252 GA, v253 GA, v254 GA} = 0 OG); **no override** — (b) cleanly STRONG. ⚠️ **Audit now 42 ships overdue** (last v212), and it gains from this ship: the new §C alternative, the #57 fan-in record, the B1-MCP ecosystem measurement, the v253-worktree scoping correction, the new watch axis, and **method rule D39** (*to establish a structural git fact use the command whose semantics ARE the definition — `rev-list --max-parents=0` for roots, `rev-list --count` for counts, never `log | head` or `log | wc`*).

### §F — v255 HeadFirstAndroid (`dogriffiths/HeadFirstAndroid`) — 2026-08-20 — **NO MINT**

**Counts UNCHANGED: 46 confirmed patterns / 11 CONFIRMED Library-vocab. §C live standalones 51 unchanged. Tracked surface ≈58 unchanged. Max pattern #85. No N-bumps taken.**

**Subject.** The official companion code to O'Reilly's *Head First Android Development*, **1st edition** (2015, Java, ISBN 1449362184). 14 Android apps across 13 chapter dirs; 578 files; 2,678 Java + 3,011 XML lines; MIT; sole author David Griffiths (152/152 commits). Source-verified by two clones, `diff -rq` clean both ways, HEAD `8327403`, `master` tip **2017-05-23**, most recent commit anywhere **2018-10-16**. Page-stated 505★ / 313 forks. **GOAL-ALIGNED INCLUDE 3/4** [(a) FAIL §41 · (b) **MODERATE** · (c) STRONG · (d) STRONG].

**NO MINT — four grounds, and the first is decisive.**

1. **Domain-not-capability (DECISIVE).** §C vocab is tool/capability-shaped. A textbook code-companion is a **domain**. The precedent chain is settled and quoted: mlsysbook **v197** — *"a single-domain educational curriculum is not a recurring capability class; §C vocab is tool/capability-shaped"* — plus AI-For-Beginners **v191**, little-book-rl **v220**, LLMs-from-scratch **v74**. And the T3-education sub-archetype candidates **v111/v113 were RETIRED at the v151 audit** under §28.3 after ~40 wikis at N=1, which forecloses re-opening the tier.
2. **Form-factor-within-a-genre.** The *"Progressive-Snapshot Curriculum Repository"* reading (the same app shipped at N successive chapter stages as separate directories) is a pedagogical form factor, not a capability, and is **not world-first** — the claude-cookbooks **v102** cookbook-not-minted precedent applies directly.
3. **Status-not-capability, and factually narrowed by this ship's own measurement.** *"Legacy Build Artifact Whose Sole Blocker Is a Repository-Coordinate Change"* describes a condition rather than a class — and the measurement refutes its own premise: it is **not** a coordinate change, because `com.android.tools.build:gradle:2.3.2` is **404 on every live public repository** (Google Maven, Maven Central, `maven.google.com`, `repo.maven.apache.org`, `plugins.gradle.org/m2`), so the plugin must be *upgraded*, not *relocated*.
4. **Rule-not-pattern.** *"Load-Bearing Stale Label"* is a portable **D-class rule**, not a §C standalone. Recorded below, not minted.

**§28 anti-inflation** independently blocks all four at **51** live standalones. **Pattern #68 (Awesome-List-Genre) is out of scope** — this is not a list, index, registry or directory.

**Instance effects on CONFIRMED patterns (all recorded, none self-incremented).**

| Pattern | Effect | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| **#12** agent-facing surface | ⭐ **NEGATIVE — the cleanest instance in the corpus** | grep of all **578** tracked filenames for `CLAUDE\|AGENTS.md\|.cursor\|copilot\|llms.txt\|SKILL\|CONTRIBUTING\|.github` → **0**. No CI on any branch, ever, in 11 years. No build instructions of any kind. |
| **#83** honest-deficiency disclosure | ⭐ **NEGATIVE** | README discloses nothing about edition, age, target API or build viability — 1 grep hit, and it is "API level 21" inside chapter-14 *content* prose. ⭐⭐ **And Android itself supplies the disclosure at every launch**: *"This app was built for an older version of Android…"* |
| **#66** supply chain | **negative data-point** (victim, not exemplar) | `jcenter()` only in all 28 `build.gradle`; removed `compile` configuration; **14 committed `gradle-wrapper.jar` binaries whose provenance is NOT ESTABLISHED** and which `./gradlew` executes. |
| **#57** corpus recursion | ⭐ **ZERO — and that is the finding** | 0 hits across **5,047,488 chars** of vault state for `HeadFirstAndroid`, `Head First`, `dogriffiths`, `Griffiths`, `AsyncTask`, `gradle`, `jcenter`. Corpus-**FIRST** Android-domain subject, **FIRST** book/textbook code-companion, **FIRST** Gradle/JVM-Android build subject. |
| **#52** viral velocity | **N/A** | API mocked (§37.4); 505★/313 forks page-stated. |
| **#19 / #88** | not applicable | single-author single-purpose companion repo; not a curation/design artifact. |

**RECORDED FOR THE AUDIT, NOT SELF-EXECUTED.**

- ⭐⭐⭐ **D40 candidate, extending D32: *a stale label is safe when DECLARED and dangerous when merely COMPENSATED* — and a declaration is itself a claim, so check its number too.** The subject's README maps chapter 7 → `chap10img.png` and chapter 11 → `chap07img.png`. It reads as an off-by-N bug; **it is correct** (verified by opening the images: `chap10img.png` is fragments-on-a-tablet = ch7; `chap07img.png` is the SQLite helper = ch11). The filenames are relics of a pre-publication numbering and the README compensates at the reference site — **undeclared**, so the naive "fix" breaks all 14 images. This is an **independent, cross-domain N=2 on the D32 phenomenon** (v245, from `unslothai/unsloth`'s `BUDGET.md`): the same disease, one **declared** fix (the vault's `_state/03c`, wrong for 71 ships and harmless because declared) and one **undeclared** one (Griffiths', correct today and fragile forever). ⚠️ Whether this is a D-class rule addition or something larger is an **audit** decision; a rule addition is an audit act.
- ⭐⭐ **DISPLACEMENT vs DIVERGENCE — offered as an audit framing, not a mint.** v250→v254 were five variations on one story: a missing, misaimed or unenforced **check**, and an artifact that drifted from its own claims. **v255 inverts it.** This subject has **no checks at all and did not drift** — not one byte since 2017-05-23; the curriculum is coherent, links resolve, images load, the chapter mapping is right. Measured against its own claims it is **more faithful than any of the last five subjects**, and it is nearly unusable. **Divergence is defended by a gate; displacement cannot be gated at all** — there is no predicate over these 578 files that is true in 2017 and false in 2026, because the bytes are identical and only their meaning changed (JCenter retired, Google relocated its Maven coordinates and abandoned AGP 2.x, Android made permissions run-time → channels mandatory → an install floor of 24). The only defence is duller than a gate: **date your work.**
- ⭐⭐⭐ **THIRD CORRECTION TO THE v246 `silent`-DETECTOR RULE.** `grep -rni "silent"` → **0**, third consecutive null, **and I ran it**. v253 explained its null as *"the detector's domain is artifacts that RUN"*; v254 refuted that (it had a runtime and still returned zero) and proposed *"artifacts whose authors have DEBUGGED them"*; **Griffiths debugged these apps page by page — the commit log proves it — and the grep is still zero.** ⇒ **the detector finds artifacts whose authors WROTE DOWN their reasoning about failure. Nothing more.** And here the null sits directly on top of a **genuine silent failure** that was **unforeseeable**: at `targetSdk ≥ 26` the notification chapter posts nothing (`E NotificationService: No Channel found for pkg=com.hfad.joke, channelId=null`), no crash, one logcat line — and channels were introduced **three months after the last commit**.

**`inflation_check` HELD.** 0 mints; all four §C alternatives recorded and declined on grounds 1–4 + §28; counts **46/11** unchanged; §C live standalones **51** unchanged; surface **≈58** unchanged; **Tier T3 Education**, joining v74 / v191 / v197 / v220.

Method note for the audit: **the adversary-over-critic stage upheld the one allegation that was checkable and false**, because it was handed the drafts but not the routine text. Next refinement: give it the governing rules too.

**FIVE GROUNDS:** (1) 🔴 **domain-not-capability, DECISIVE** — media localization is a *domain* and §C vocabulary is tool/capability-shaped: **v197 mlsysbook** (*"a single-domain educational curriculum is not a recurring capability class"*), **v196 meetily** (corpus-first for a DOMAIN ≠ mintable), **v210 AIRI**, **v220 little-book-rl**, **v211 PixelRAG** (technique-not-capability); (2) 🔴 **not world-first and not canonical** — the staged, GUI-driven, manually-correctable comic translator is an established category with far larger incumbents, and under **v222** (*world-canonical is not world-first*) this anchor claims neither at **32★ / 62 commits / 14 days / one author**; (3) 🔴 **the differentiating machinery is not this repository's** — every capability that makes the product work is a third-party model (PP-DocLayoutV3, Manga RT-DETR, DeepSeek-OCR, PaddleOCR-VL, LaMa-manga) and a meaningful slice of the code was **imported wholesale in commit #2** ⇒ **v242 D25** applied to the anchor itself; (4) 🔴 **form-factor-within-a-genre** — a desktop GUI over an existing pipeline category (the **v102** cookbook precedent; the **v236 / v227 / v222** front-end chain); (5) 🔴 **§28 anti-inflation** at **51 live standalones**, ≤2/ship, clustering-first, against a bus-factor-one anchor (**v180 / v234** weak-anchor precedent). **Any one of grounds 1–3 is sufficient.**

**⭐⭐⭐ THE HEADLINE — the second commit imported a different program, and the dependency file still describes it.** `bdcb098`, **five minutes fifty-two seconds after `Initial commit`**, is **68 files / 8,675 insertions** and it is a **Flask web application** (`app.py`, `templates/`, `static/`, `.dockerignore`, a trained `model/model.pt`, its training notebook, **`.jules/bolt.md`**); the repo is **not a fork** and **no attribution file has ever existed**. The web app was deleted in ONE commit (`a41abd7 "Clean new"`, 2026-05-18) — **which did not touch `requirements.txt`, whose entire history is six commits, all four days earlier.** ⇒ **10 of 24 requirements have no direct importer across all 152 `.py` files** (`Flask`/`flask-socketio`/`python-socketio`/`python-engineio`/`Werkzeug`/`gunicorn`/`tqdm`/`cryptography`/`sentencepiece`/`huggingface-hub` all **0**), and `gunicorn`'s own PyPI classifiers list only MacOS/POSIX — a UNIX-only server pinned into a Windows-`.bat`-only project. ⭐⭐ **The rule: deleting a program is not deleting its dependencies, and a dependency list is the last place anybody looks for dead code** — and **nothing could notice, because no `.yml`/`.yaml` has ever existed on any ref.**

**⭐⭐⭐ THE BEST FINDING — the agent's memory was deleted; the fix it prescribed is still running.** `.jules/bolt.md` (Jules, Google's async coding agent) held one dated `**Learning:**`/`**Action:**` entry prescribing **`_model_cache`** class attributes or a global registry. Deleted 2026-05-18 — and **both halves survive at HEAD, named exactly as the agent named them**: `translator/translator.py:43` (with a **migration note** at `:38`) and `detect_bubbles.py:6`. ⇒ **two comments referring to a decision whose reasoning has been deleted from the working tree.** **v255's D40 from the opposite direction:** there a *stale* label was safe **because declared**; here a *correct* mechanism is fragile **because its declaration was thrown away** — and the inverse of **D22**: this prose did not go stale, **it was deleted while still accurate**, which is worse, because staleness is detectable and absence is not.

**⭐⭐⭐ THE GOOD DESIGN, and the synthesis of six ships — a check declared, persisted, displayed, and deliberately NOT enforced.** Three constants (`detection_edit.py:29`, `ocr_edit.py:23`, `render_edit.py:29`) declare each edit's blast radius, and `detection_edit.py:107,154` write **`payload["downstream_stale"] = list(DOWNSTREAM_STALE_STAGES)`** into the cached JSON ⇒ **the mark is persisted IN THE ARTIFACT, machine-readable, naming the stages**, surviving process exit, **not inferred from `mtime`** (v244 **D29** avoided); the GUI renders it as a warning. **Then it stops: 36 mentions, 5 write sites, ZERO enforcement** — and **the README says so** in troubleshooting §5. ⇒ v250 called six rules *"non-negotiable"* and gated one · v252 headed a block *"Automated, Zero Tolerance"* and automated none · v247 committed a gate its own history violates 66 of 82 times · v254 aimed a weekly job at the one thing nobody doubted · **v256 has an unenforced check and claims nothing else.** ⭐ **RULE: an unenforced check is a defect only when something claims it is enforced.** ⚠️ Its real defect is **v240's inventory rule** — the stage DAG is declared four+ times with nothing reconciling them, and the three lists don't share a convention.

**⭐⭐⭐ THE SYNTHESIS (D44 candidate, generalising v255's D32/D40) — FOUR ARTIFACTS OUTLIVED THE CONTEXT THAT PRODUCED THEM, AND ONE CARRIES ITS OWN.** (1) `requirements.txt` — context was `app.py`, deleted 18 May. (2) `_model_cache` + its comments — context was `.jules/bolt.md`, deleted the same day. (3) `servers/paddleocr_vl/run_server.bat` is **committed GENERATED OUTPUT** — `llama_server.py:198` is `bat_path.write_text(self.build_bat_content(), …)` and the committed file has exactly the generator's shape (one token per line, `" ^"` continuations, a lone `--port ^`) while hardcoding four absolute paths under `C:\Nghich\Manga-Translator\`; **not gitignored** ⇒ *the project mechanises the launcher so port and paths cannot drift, then committed one machine-specific instance of the output.* ⚠️ This corrected **both** my first reading (a hand-written launcher) **and** a fleet agent's (a hand-duplicated port) — its conclusion wrong, its instinct right. (4) ⭐⭐⭐ **`detectors/comic_text_detector.py` CANNOT BE IMPORTED** — `:9` imports `assign_text_regions_to_bubbles` (called at `:456`) and `matching.py` defines six functions, none of them that one; it existed at `617d044` (`matching.py:143`) and `git log -S` puts its removal in `2aebd41 "Remove CTD"` (2026-05-17); the symbol now appears **exactly twice in the tree, both inside the file that cannot resolve it** ⇒ **`ImportError` at load, LATENT because nothing imports it.** ⭐⭐ **That is what "no CI, ever" costs in one line: a 152-module project with a module unimportable since 17 May, and one `python -c "import …"` would have caught it that day.** **The counter-example is in the same repository: `downstream_stale` carries its own context** — written into the artifact it invalidates, naming the invalidated stages machine-readably, stated by the GUI to the person who must act. ⇒ **AN ARTIFACT IS SAFE WHEN IT CARRIES ITS OWN CONTEXT, AND DANGEROUS WHEN ITS CONTEXT LIVED SOMEWHERE ELSE AND HAS SINCE GONE.** Declaring a lag is one *instance* of carrying context — this names what "declared" buys — and it identifies the failure mode **worse than staleness: a deleted rationale leaves an artifact currently correct and unmaintainable.** ⚠️ **The vault's own instance is the CLAUSE-1 FAIL this ship closed.**

**RECORDED, NOT SELF-INCREMENTED:** **#83** POSITIVE ×2 (the Google-Lens IP-ban disclosure; the self-documented dead root scripts) ⚠️ **and the same README is simultaneously a #83 NEGATIVE** on a different feature (an advertised OCR provider that does not exist and cannot, since `OCRConfig` has no `api_key` field) — ***one document, both poles*, worth an audit note** · **#12** a clean **NEGATIVE richer than v255's** — the agent surface was **inherited and deleted**, not merely absent · **#66** a strong **NEGATIVE exemplar** (two unsatisfiable pins per PyPI's own `requires_dist`, 10 zero-importer requirements, 20+ advisories, no lockfile) — the counter-pole to **v228 pi** · **#19** the author runs a small Vietnamese content-localization portfolio incl. **`translate-LN-pipeline`**, the *text* sibling to this *image* tool · **NOT #52** · ⚠️ **THE v232-ROW BOUNDARY, AND I DECLINE TO STRETCH IT:** the §C standalone minted at **v232** (crediting **v231**) is scoped to a self-hosted **GATEWAY that RE-EXPOSES** a free consumer entitlement **as an API**; this subject is an **end-user application that CONSUMES** Google Lens directly and **re-exposes nothing** ⇒ an **ADJACENCY, not an instance**, and generalising that row to "any application calling a free consumer endpoint" would silently fire its own promotion arithmetic. ### §F — v257 translate-LN-pipeline (`mranex/translate-LN-pipeline`) — 2026-08-21 — **NO MINT**

**Subject.** *"Manual Studio v3"* — a PyQt6 desktop workbench for translating Chinese web novels into Vietnamese, README entirely in Vietnamese. ⭐ **The corpus's first genuine SAME-AUTHOR CONTROL:** the text sibling of the v256 subject by the same author, requested by the operator; **17 days older** and ending **the same day** (v256 11:39, this 17:13). Source-verified (two clones, `diff -rq` clean both ways), HEAD `23dd4704`; **11 commits, ONE root, ZERO merges, ONE tag named "Windows", 88 files, 47 `.py` / 12,561 lines, 18 `.pyc` TRACKED, three git names over one email**; 7★/3 forks page-stated (§37.4 ⇒ **NOT #52**); ⚠️ **NO LICENCE AT ALL** (0 of 88 files, 0 in 11 commits, 0 headers in 47 `.py`, 0 README mentions). GOAL-ALIGNED INCLUDE 3/4 [(a) FAIL §41 · (b) **STRONG** ⚠️MODERATE-reviewable · (c) STRONG · (d) STRONG]; **§40 applies** — no override, no §35 pressure.

**Counts UNCHANGED: 46 confirmed patterns / 11 CONFIRMED Library-vocab. §C live standalones 51 unchanged. Tracked surface ≈58 unchanged. Max pattern #85. No N-bumps taken.**

**⭐ STRONGEST ALTERNATIVE — recorded, reviewable, NOT self-executed:** a **§C standalone at N=1, *"Prompt-Rendering Workbench That Deliberately Does Not Call the API — the Human Carries the Prompt to a Chat LLM and Pastes the Response Back."*** The most interesting candidate in several ships, because it is **the honest pole of an economic pressure the corpus has already minted the dishonest pole of** — **v207/v208** re-expose CLI-tool OAuth subscriptions as APIs, **v231/v232** drive consumer chat UIs to harvest a free entitlement, and **all four automate the subscription while this one refuses to and puts a human in the transport instead.**

**FIVE GROUNDS:** (1) 🔴 **domain-not-capability** (**v197** *"a single-domain educational curriculum is not a recurring capability class"*; **v196**/**v210**/**v220**); (2) 🔴 **the distinctive property is an ABSENCE** — a class defined by what the tool declines to do; its positive description (*renders a prompt into a text box, parses what you paste back*) is a **form factor** ⇒ the **v102** cookbook precedent and the **v236/v227/v222** front-end chain; (3) 🔴 **not world-first and not canonical** — structured copy-paste LLM workflows and prompt-template tools long predate it, and under **v222** a 7★/11-commit/88-file repo claims neither; (4) 🔴 **§28** at **51 live standalones**, ≤2/ship, clustering-first, against a **bus-factor-one** anchor with three git identities and no licence (**v180/v234**); (5) 🔴 **the sibling was declined ONE DAY EARLIER on five grounds** (v256) — minting this while declining that requires a distinction stronger than *"the LLM is a larger share of this one,"* which is a difference of degree.

**⭐ RECORDED FOR THE AUDIT — a DEFERRED WATCH AXIS: *"manual / human-transport LLM workflow — a tool built so the operator's chat subscription substitutes for API billing, with no automation of the transport."*** The counterpart to the **v207/v208/v231/v232 gateway cluster**, and a **boundary question** against those rows. Recorded, not executed — a promotion is an audit act.

**⭐⭐⭐ THE HEADLINE — the README's primary launch command starts the wrong program.** Three complete generations are tracked at HEAD: (1) `run.py` → `python -m src.main` (**Tkinter**, 13 buttons, 17 verbs, **the only generation that calls the API**); (2) `manual_prompt_studio.py` (52,295 bytes, **Tkinter**, self-labelled `APP='Manual Prompt Studio v2'`, reachable from nothing, yet importing from the *newer* package); (3) `manual_studio/` + `qt_app.py` (**PyQt6**, "Version 3.0", launched by `run.bat`). **The README documents only generation 3** and its primary instruction is **`python run.py`** — which opens generation 1 — with `run.bat` as the parenthetical. ⭐ And generation 1's most prominent button targets **`ln_pipeline_final_editor.py`, never committed in any of the 11 commits**, with a handled Vietnamese not-found message ⇒ **the headline button of the program the README tells you to run fails for everyone.**

**⭐⭐⭐ ONE POLICY, THREE THRESHOLDS, AND THEY DISAGREE.** `config/config.json` declares `review_confidence_threshold: 0.72` / `auto_accept_confidence_threshold: 0.82` plus **four** `force_review_if_*` rules — and is read **only** by `src/config_loader.py:6` and `src/main.py:19`, i.e. **generation 1**, while generation 3 reads `project_config.json` (`workspace.py:22–27`). `manual_workflow.py:117–118` and `manual_prompt_studio.py:528` hardcode **0.72/0.82** and inject them **into the prompt** ⇒ the *model* is told those numbers. `review_flags.py:101,104` and `editor_actions.py:455` enforce **`confidence < 0.8`**. ⇒ **the model is told 0.72/0.82, the app enforces 0.8, and the file that declares the canonical pair is not read by the app at all — three copies, and the enforced value matches NEITHER declared value.** The four `force_review_if_*` rules appear in **ZERO** of the 47 `.py` files. ⚠️ **Not "declared and unimplemented"** — `review_flags.py` is a real 139-line service emitting `ReviewFlag(severity, source, item_id, message, payload)`; **the defect is triplication with disagreement, which is worse than absence.** ⚠️ **Adversary's steelman recorded and answered:** telling the *model* the thresholds is legitimate in a render-and-paste design, but declaration 3 *does* gate in code at an unpublished value, and the four conditions are **rules, not thresholds**, absent from prompt and code alike ⇒ **a design that delegates to the human still has to tell somebody the rule.**

**⭐⭐⭐ THE GATE THAT CANNOT FIRE.** `artifact_store.py:25` writes `{"item_id": …, "status": "success", "result": …}` — **a literal, assigned at write time.** Two release paths gate on it (`release_builder.py:354,364`; `release_service.py:140,150`, both `!= "success"`). **The only writer of `"failed"` is `src/pipeline.py:28` — generation 1.** ⇒ **in the generation that ships, the sole writer of that field is a constant, so a correctly-written, twice-consumed gate can never exclude anything.** ⭐⭐ **THE RULE: a gate can outlive its signal source** — v250 aimed a gate at the wrong thing, v252 labelled unautomated rules *"Automated"*, v256 kept artifacts whose context had been deleted, **v257 keeps a gate whose only varying writer belongs to a generation that is no longer the product.**

**⭐⭐⭐ THE DURABILITY CLAIM AND ITS READER (the fleet's best find).** The README's section headed *"…Khôi Phục **Siêu Bền Bỉ**"* argues for JSONL rows precisely so *one line's error cannot ruin the whole file*. `jsonio.read_jsonl` is a **list comprehension with a bare `json.loads` and no per-row `try`** ⇒ 🔴 **one malformed line loses EVERY row**, and `artifact_store`, `progress`, `review_flags` and `editor_index` all read through it. ⚠️ The README's own example row says `"status": "done"` while the code writes `"success"` and both gates test `!= "success"` ⇒ **the documented value would be silently skipped by the gate that reads it.**

**⭐⭐ WHAT IS GENUINELY GOOD.** ⭐⭐⭐ **ONE ESCALATION LOOP IS FULLY CLOSED AND THE MODEL OPENS IT:** `prompts/03…txt:11` asks the model to put important-but-unglossed terms in **`missing_glossary_candidates`** *for human review*; `review_flags.py` reads exactly that field and raises a warning the UI shows. **Prompt, schema and gate agree on one string** — the best thing here, and the pattern the three thresholds failed to achieve. ⭐⭐ **The prompts are strong** (role, `{{genre}}`, explicit scope fences, a **precedence rule** where existing canon outranks new guesses, a **conflict protocol** that marks contradictions instead of overwriting, and two-tier `confirmed`/`tentative` semantics that tell the model **how a downstream model will consume the field**). ⭐⭐ **Every canon entry carries its own provenance** (`source_items` per variant + `appears_in`) ⇒ **the v256 "artifact carries its own context" rule SATISFIED by the same author who violated it four times next door.** ✅ Clean secrets posture (`"api_key_env"`, `.env.example`, `.gitignore`), a correct quote/escape-aware JSON recovery scanner, disk-derived progress, a safe list-arg Calibre call that inspects `returncode`, and `series_canon` appending + logging conflicts rather than overwriting.

**⭐⭐⭐ THE SAME-AUTHOR CONTROL — the ship's real contribution.** **REPLICATES (N=2):** ignored-yet-tracked files · one fact declared many times with nothing reconciling · committed artifacts carrying the author's own machine paths · dead generations left in the tree · **no CI/tests/agent surface in either** (clean **#12 negatives** ×2) · an unbenchmarked headline number in both. **DIVERGES:** `requirements.txt` (24 lines **unsatisfiable** vs **3 clean lines**) · response validation (**five layers** vs **none**) · README screenshots (**never existed** vs **both exist**) · `shell=True` (**0 in 152 files** vs **2 in 47**) · licence (**AGPL** vs **none**). ⚠️ **CORRECTION TO MY OWN GROUND TRUTH:** I asserted the ignore-rule mechanism was identical; it is not — v256's `.gitignore` and its tracked exception arrived in **one commit** (`bdcb098`), while here the 18 `.pyc` came with the **root commit** and the `.gitignore` **26 days later** (`7a590cb`) ⇒ **the author wrote a rule naming `__pycache__/` while 18 tracked `.pyc` sat in the tree and never removed them. What replicates is the OUTCOME, not the route** — and two unrelated routes to the same dead artifact is better evidence of a disposition than one repeated slip. ⭐⭐⭐ **AND THE ORDERING SETTLES IT: this repo's screenshots landed 2026-05-22; the sibling's README was last edited 2026-05-23 — the day after — and still points at two images that never existed. Not a learning curve. Two projects side by side, one tended and one not.** ⇒ ⭐⭐⭐ **THE FINDING: THE HABITS ARE THE CONSTANT; THE QUALITY IS THE VARIABLE.** Everything replicating is **bookkeeping**; everything diverging is **care applied to a particular surface**. **Discipline attaches to whatever the developer was thinking about; structure fails everywhere else.**

**RECORDED, NOT SELF-INCREMENTED:** **#83 POSITIVE *and* NEGATIVE in one document, second consecutive ship** (POSITIVE: the README's development-status NOTE openly admits the `review_*` steps are registered placeholders actually performed in the Editor tab; NEGATIVE: the unmeasured 80%, the misattributed `.bak`, the JSONL contradiction, the wrong launch command) · **#12** clean NEGATIVE, **same-author N=2** · **#66** a MIXED exemplar, useful beside **v256** (negative) and **v228 pi** (positive) · **#19** the author archetype's second data point · **NOT #52** · **Tier** application/client, reviewable.

### §F — v258 VOCra (`mranex/VOCra`) — 2026-08-21 — **NO MINT**

**Subject.** *"VoCRA & VoCRA Translator"* — a PySide6 desktop toolchain extracting **hardcoded (burned-in) subtitles** from video (region crop → SSIM frame dedup → local-VLM OCR → LLM translation → export → optional ffmpeg re-burn), plus a second app for SRT/ASS/VTT files. README Vietnamese. ⭐ **The THIRD `mranex` repo in three consecutive ships** (manga **v256** → novels **v257** → video **v258**), turning the v257 same-author control from **N=2 into N=3** — the ship's actual contribution, and **it corrected me twice**. Source-verified (two clones, `diff -rq` clean both ways), HEAD `9cc598bb`; **6 commits, ONE root, ZERO merges, ZERO tags, 93 files, 75 `.py` / 9,641 lines, 10 `.pyc` TRACKED, no `.gitignore` in the tree**; window **2026-05-26 → 2026-05-28 ≈ 2.4 days**; 3★/0 forks page-stated (§37.4 ⇒ **NOT #52**); ⚠️ **MIT claimed in the README, no licence file, and no dependency manifest at all.** GOAL-ALIGNED INCLUDE 3/4 [(a) FAIL §41 · (b) **MODERATE** ⚠️OFF-GOAL-reviewable · (c) STRONG · (d) STRONG]; **§40 applies** — no override, no §35 pressure.

**Counts UNCHANGED: 46 confirmed patterns / 11 CONFIRMED Library-vocab. §C live standalones 51 unchanged. Tracked surface ≈58 unchanged. Max pattern #85. No N-bumps taken.**

**Declined §C standalone at N=1:** *"Burned-In Subtitle Extraction Pipeline: region crop → SSIM frame deduplication → local VLM OCR → LLM translation, in one desktop workbench."* **FIVE GROUNDS:** (1) 🔴 **domain-not-capability** (**v197**/**v196**/**v210**/**v220**); (2) 🔴 **not world-first nor canonical** — burned-in subtitle OCR is long-established (VideoSubFinder, `videocr`), and under **v222** a **3★/6-commit/2.4-day** repo claims neither; (3) 🔴 **§28** at **51 live standalones** against **the weakest anchor of the three siblings** (bus factor one, no manifest, no licence file — **v180/v234**); (4) 🔴 **form-factor-within-a-genre** (**v102**; the **v236/v227/v222** front-end chain); (5) 🔴 **both siblings were declined on five grounds each, one and two days earlier** — minting the third of three needs a distinction that does not exist. **Any of 1–3 suffices. `inflation_check` HELD.**

**⭐ RECORDED FOR THE AUDIT — deliberately NOT phrased as mints:**

- ⭐⭐⭐ **A METHOD NOTE — the same-author control.** When the operator requests consecutive repositories by one author, **the comparison is worth more than either subject**: three points separate a developer's **dispositions** (what replicates regardless of project) from their **attention** (what varies with what they were looking at). **The corpus's first N=3 of this kind, and the shape is reusable — it belongs in the routine's METHOD section, not in §C.**
- ⭐⭐ **An observation, not a capability: "code moves forward in time, never backward."** In a solo multi-project toolchain an improvement invented in project N reaches N+1 by copy-forward and never reaches N−1, because no shared library, package, dependency or CI carries it back. **Evidence below.** An argument for extracting shared libraries; not §C vocabulary.
- ⭐⭐ **"A plan document is a fence around exactly the surfaces it names."** Measured below.

**⭐⭐⭐ THE HEADLINE — one commit deleted the application, the ignore rule, and 252KB of its own reasoning.** Commit 2 added a layered app (`app/` service layer, `cli/`, `gui/` with a 93KB main window, `_version.py`); commit 3, **one minute later**, added `docs/Plan.md` (1,178 lines) and `docs/progress.md` (**2,064 lines / 232,097 B**). **Eight hours later `7e5f67d` "Big update 1": 223 files changed, 7,158 insertions, 20,339 deletions — 89 deletions including `.gitignore`, the whole service layer, the whole CLI, the whole GUI and both documents — plus 80 additions of `.pyc`.** ⭐⭐⭐ **The `.gitignore` it removed had `__pycache__/` on line 2, so THE COMMIT THAT DELETED THE IGNORE RULE IS THE COMMIT THAT ADDED THE FIRST 80 FILES IT NAMED.** ⚠️ **Not a story about shipping junk** — the *result* is the current cleaner architecture, and on several axes this is the **best-built of the three siblings**. It is a story about **what survives a rewrite**.

**⭐⭐⭐ 262KB OF REASONING WRITTEN IN 2.4 DAYS, 98.7% DELETED, AND THE SURVIVOR IS THE ONLY DECISION THE CODE HONOURS.** Four planning docs: `docs/progress.md` 2,064 lines (**deleted same day**) · `docs/Plan.md` 1,178 lines (**deleted same day**) · `total_plan.md` 157 lines (**deleted next day**) · `vocra_translator/plan.md` 75 lines (✅ **kept**). **Written 262,074 B / 3,474 lines; surviving 3,306 B / 75 lines = 1.26% by bytes** — and the chain is a ladder, each rung an order of magnitude smaller, **the smallest the only survivor.** `docs/progress.md` carried the eleven headings of an AI assistant's working-memory file (*Current Goal · Completed · Blockers / Risks · Architecture Decisions · Next Recommended Steps* …) for a project three hours old; ⚠️ **it names no tool** (grep for every common assistant name → nothing) ⇒ **shape is evidence, provenance is not stated**. ⭐⭐⭐ **The 75-line survivor states that the second app REUSES `vocra_core.translator.*` — and the code honours it** (`vocra_translator/core/provider_factory.py:3–5` imports its three translator classes from there): **the only place in three repositories where a written decision demonstrably shaped the code.** ⭐⭐⭐ **AND IT FENCED EXACTLY THE SURFACE IT NAMED:** the plan is silent on widgets, so `scene_nav_button.py` is **BYTE-IDENTICAL (117 lines) in both apps**, `log_panel.py` differs by 2 lines, `theme.qss` by 14, and **`icon.png` is byte-identical at 788,588 bytes and committed TWICE** (~1.5MB of a 3.9MB clone). ⇒ **A plan document is a fence around the surfaces it names. Name the boring ones too.**

**⭐⭐⭐ THE N=3 CONTROL.** **Six habits replicate:** ignored-yet-tracked files (**three distinct routes** — v256 rule+exception in one commit; v257 files 26 days before the rule; **v258 rule deleted by the commit that added the files**) · one fact declared many times (v256 DAG 3+ files; v257 threshold ×3 **disagreeing**; **v258 SSIM `0.95` ×8, all agreeing** — ⚠️ I counted 6, a fleet agent found the two GUI copies; and `scene_config.py:190` is a **reset-to-default that hardcodes the value** rather than re-reading it) · committed machine paths (`C:\Nghich\Manga-Translator\` / `C:\Users\Admin\Desktop\` / **`C:\Nghich\vocra\`** — and **N=2 in the identical form**, both generated `.bat` files from the same generator design) · dead generations (✅ **after correction** — see below) · **no CI/tests/agent surface in all three** (0 `.yml` ever; 0 tests of 93; 0 agent files of 93 ⇒ three clean **#12 negatives**) · an unbenchmarked headline number in all three. **Diverging axes:** `shell=True` **0/152 · 2/47 · 0/75** · alignment validation **five layers · NONE · present** · README images **never existed · working · a `your-username` placeholder that 404s** · licence **AGPL file · none · MIT in prose** · dependency manifest **24 lines with two unsatisfiable pins · 3 clean lines · NONE**.

**⚠️⚠️ MY TWO CORRECTIONS, both improving the ship.** 🔴 **(1)** Habit 4 nearly shipped as *"does not replicate"*: my tree-level import trace found zero unimported modules **and I sanity-tested the detector against a planted name** — right discipline, wrong target. `git rev-list --objects --all | git cat-file --batch-check` then exposed a deleted 93KB GUI, a deleted `app/` layer, a deleted `cli/` and 252KB of deleted docs ⇒ **the tree is clean, the history is not** ⇒ **NEW RULE D46: a clean tree is not a clean history — enumerate the pack before concluding a repo has no dead generations.** 🔴 **(2)** I attributed `plan.md` to v257; it is **VOCra's own file**, and `git ls-files '*.md'` in all three clones shows **v257 has exactly one markdown file, its README, and has NEVER had a planning document.** ⚠️ **I had written the wrong attribution into the fleet's ground-truth block before catching it — the v257 amplifier hazard, repeating one ship later.** ⭐⭐⭐ **And the correction made a three-point correlation measurable: v257 wrote ZERO planning docs → README launches the wrong program, three disagreeing thresholds, two `shell=True`, no alignment guard; v256 wrote ONE (inherited, deleted) → four context-less artifacts, an unimportable module; v258 wrote FOUR (one survives) → the surviving decision honoured, zero `shell=True`, the guard present, a real provider abstraction.** ⇒ **the repo with the most written-down reasoning is the best-built, the repo with none is the worst-built.** ⚠️ **N=3 from one author is a hypothesis, not a law, and the ship says so** — but the mechanism is legible: a plan fences the surfaces it names.

**⭐⭐⭐ CODE MOVES FORWARD, NEVER BACKWARD.** The per-item alignment guard was **INVENTED in v256** at `67af566` (**2026-05-15 23:38**, subject `New`) as *"OpenAI-compatible response item index mismatch at position…"*; **CARRIED into v258** (`vocra_core/translator/openai_compatible.py:173,181`, *"Translator item index mismatch at position…"*, plus two further paths) in a repo created **eleven days later**; and **NEVER REACHED v257**, which has **zero** alignment guards across all 47 `.py` (grep exit 1) despite being committed to **five times on 2026-05-22 and once on 2026-05-27 17:13** — all after the guard existed, the last on the final recorded day of all three repos. ⚠️ **Steelman, partly holding:** v257 is render-and-paste with a *human* transport, so a literal port is harder and its button honestly says *check syntax*; **answered** — it renders a numbered batch and imports a JSON reply, so **the count IS checkable and is not checked** ⇒ **the barrier is not architecture alone; nothing in a copy-forward workflow ever runs in reverse.**

**⭐⭐ THE CARRIED-OVER SUBSYSTEM GOT BETTER AND ITS BUG RODE ALONG.** v256's OCR layer: 5 ad-hoc files / **1,342 lines**, no shared base. v258's `final_ocr/`: 8 files / **971 lines**, with a **19-line `FinalOCRProvider` Protocol** and a **17-line factory that raises on unknown** ⇒ **same subsystem, third project, 28% smaller and an actual interface.** 🔴 **And `subprocess.Popen(["sh", str(bat_path)])` — the POSIX shell on a Windows `.bat` — is present in both, unchanged** (v256 `llama_server.py:221`; v258 `llama_server_manager.py:131`). ⚠️ A fleet agent tried to dismiss it as *"harmless; bash ignores the .sh extension"* — **rejected: the issue is the batch CONTENT, not the extension.** ⇒ ⭐⭐ **Structure improves on a carried-forward surface; bugs ride along** — refactoring moves the shape and copies the content, and a defect inside a function body is content.

**⭐⭐ GENUINELY GOOD — best of the three on several axes:** ✅ **ZERO `shell=True`/`os.system`** across 75 files (grep run alone, exit 1); 8 `subprocess` sites, all argv-list. ✅ **ZERO hardcoded HTTP(S) URLs in any `.py`** — endpoints come from `default_config.json` with empty `base_url`/`api_key` defaults; **neither sibling managed this.** ✅ **Secrets deliberate:** separate `secrets.json`, a `VOCRA_TRANSLATOR_API_KEY` env override, and **`app_config.py:52` pops `api_key` before saving settings**; `secrets.json` never tracked in any commit — ⚠️ though now protected by nothing but luck, since the `.gitignore` was deleted. ✅ A real provider abstraction + factory in both OCR and translator layers. ✅ The alignment guard in three paths. ✅ **A documented architectural decision the code honours** — the only instance across the three. ✅ Progress derived purely from files on disk; external calls use list args and inspect `returncode`.

**🔴 DEFECTS:** the README's **logo is an unfilled `your-username` template placeholder** and both it and its raw image URL return **HTTP 404** (verified by `curl`) · **two unmeasured headline claims** (100% of noise, 90%+ of duplicate frames at SSIM `0.95`) with **no counter, log or benchmark anywhere** — searched across all 75 `.py`, both `.md` and the config · 🔴 **UNDECLARED CONFIG, the v240 inventory rule firing in REVERSE and found by the fleet:** `default_config.json` declares five top-level sections and **`draft_ocr` is not among them**, yet code reads it in five places (`app_config.py:77,85`; `draft_ocr.py:49` an unguarded subscript; `scene_config.py:62,119`) ⇒ **an entire pipeline stage's settings exist only as a runtime construction** — *not* dead config (declared, unread) but **undeclared config (read, never declared)**, the direction the vault's own bidirectional check exists to catch · **10 committed `.pyc`** in `cpython-311`+`312` pairs · ⭐ **the README is load-bearing for two things convention puts in files** — the licence grant (`:169`) and the dependency set (`:129`, seven unpinned packages, no manifest of any kind) — **and it is also the file with the broken placeholder at the top.**

**ALSO RECORDED:** **#12** clean NEGATIVE, **same-author N=3** · **#66** strongest of the three on egress and secrets, **weakest on declaration** · **#83 POSITIVE and NEGATIVE in one document, third consecutive ship** · **#19** the author archetype's **third** data point · **NOT #52** · **Tier** application/client, ⚠️ reviewable.

**(b) = MODERATE, moving DOWN from v257's STRONG.** v257 rated STRONG because the LLM *was* the subject; **strip the video away here and what remains is computer vision**, with the translator package **~490 lines of 9,641**. That is v256's shape, and v256 was MODERATE. On-goal, ranked: ⭐⭐⭐ **the N=3 control method itself** · ⭐⭐ **the cheap-deterministic-filter-before-the-expensive-model pattern** (the one *new* transfer) · ⭐ the provider Protocol + factory · — the alignment guard is already banked at v256, so its value here is **evidential, not new**. ⚠️ **OFF-GOAL recorded:** a hobbyist tool with zero agent surface, 3★, no manifest. ⚠️⚠️ **PRECEDENT NOTE, FOURTH CONSECUTIVE SHIP: NO MINT and OFF-GOAL are ORTHOGONAL** — §40 names **v191** and **v197**, both NO MINT, as *"simply GOAL-ALIGNED."*

**`inflation_check` HELD** — 0 mints; the §C alternative recorded and declined on grounds 1–5; counts **46/11** unchanged; §C live standalones **51** unchanged; surface **≈58** unchanged; max pattern #85; **no N-bumps taken.**

**Streak: v257 `GA:115` → `GA:116 · OG:13 [7 ov]`** (**39 consecutive GA**, v220→v258); **§35 CLEAR** (window {v256 GA, v257 GA, v258 GA} = 0 OG); **no override** (§40). ⚠️ **Audit now 46 ships overdue** (last v212). ⭐ **Three consecutive same-author ships have produced a METHOD contribution only an audit can adopt — the strongest argument yet for making v259 the audit.** This ship also adds **D46** (a clean tree is not a clean history — enumerate the pack) and **D47** (anchor the basename in filename greps, or the test confirms whatever it looks for), and a third consecutive instance of the standing self-diagnosis: **I am reliable when I read a file and quote it, and unreliable when I generalise from where I chose to look.** ⚠️ **Fleet note:** several agents proposed edits to the SUBJECT (commit a LICENSE, remove the percentage claims, replace the placeholder) — **a category error: a wiki documents its subject, it does not repair it.**

**⭐⭐⭐ LATE ADDITIONS FROM THE FLEET, ALL THREE VERIFIED BY ME — and the first is the ship's best positive.** **(1) AN INVALIDATION MECHANISM THAT ACTUALLY ENFORCES, completing a three-point arc the corpus has circled since v250.** `segmenter.py:_invalidate_downstream_outputs()` does not warn, it acts: it **deletes** the downstream cache files (`ocr_final`, `translation`) via `cache_path.unlink()`, then resets **four** status flags (`preprocessed_done`, `ocr_final_done`, `translation_done`, `export_done`) to `False` and persists them with `save_progress`. ⇒ **v256 wrote `DOWNSTREAM_STALE_STAGES` into its cached JSON, showed a GUI warning, and enforced NOTHING; v257 had NO staleness mechanism at all; v258 DELETES THE ARTIFACTS.** ⭐⭐⭐ **The repository with the most written-down reasoning is also the only one of the three that enforces its own invalidation — an independent confirmation of the ship's correlation, from a completely unrelated surface, and found by the fleet rather than by me.** **(2) 🔴 THE TIMEOUT IS ON THE WRONG CALL.** `burner.py` invokes ffmpeg two ways: `_run_capture` (`:582–583`), a **short probe**, is `subprocess.run(…, timeout=timeout)` with `timeout` a **required keyword-only parameter**; `_run_ffmpeg_stream` (`:534`), **the long-running video encode**, is a `Popen` followed by a bare **`process.wait()` with no timeout at all** ⇒ **the call that finishes in milliseconds is guarded by a mandatory timeout and the call that can run for hours has none**, so a stalled ffmpeg hangs the encode indefinitely. **Not ignorance of the parameter — the author made it compulsory in the helper directly below** ⇒ ⭐ **the same shape as everything else in this ship: the discipline exists and was applied to the surface that did not need it.** **(3) ⚠️ THE SSIM FILTER IS GREEDY-SEQUENTIAL, WHICH BOUNDS THE 90% CLAIM.** `ssim_filter.py:78` compares each frame **only** against `last_unique_image`, the reference advancing only on a miss (`:88–89`) — no rolling history. So a subtitle that disappears and **reappears** (A → B → A) yields three "unique" frames and the second A is OCRed again. ⭐ **It is the right algorithm for the common case** (a subtitle sits still, then changes) and it under-deduplicates on repeats ⇒ **the removal rate is a property of subtitle RUN LENGTH, not of the number of distinct subtitles, so "90%+" describes the input rather than the filter.** ⚠️ **Also recorded:** `draft_ocr.py:61–76` has deduplicated frames inherit their leader's OCR text via `frame_map` **with no check that the leader succeeded**, so an empty leader silently loses the subtitle for that whole run. ⚠️ **AND ONE FLEET CLAIM REJECTED, with the reason:** an agent reported `exporter.py:152` (`centis = int(round(millis / 10.0))`, clamped to 99) as a precision defect. **It is not — ASS timestamps are `H:MM:SS.cc`, so centiseconds are the format's own resolution**; the rounding is required and the clamp is a correctness guard. Recorded because it is exactly the class of "defect" that looks real until you read the specification.

### §F — v259 AUDIT (window v213 → v258) — 2026-08-21 — ⭐ **1 PROMOTION (#24) · §C BIFURCATED · ROUTINE v2.8**

**Type:** mini-audit, 46 ships overdue (last audit v212, 2026-07-17). Doc: `04 Reviews/(C) 2026-08-21 — v259 audit (v213–v258)`. Routine delta: `05 Skills/llm-wiki-routine-v2.8.md`.

**HOLD — the v207 subscription-gateway row stays at N=2.** Its only 2nd instance (v208 OmniRoute) is an openly-credited port. **v231/v232 were considered and rejected as instances**: they harvest a *free consumer web-chat entitlement* and anchor their own row — a different entitlement, mechanism and risk profile. **v257 raised this boundary and declined to stretch it; this audit upholds that.**

**⭐⭐ COUNT RECONCILED — and the count was right; my measurement was wrong.** I asserted a one-row drift and was wrong twice (50, then 52) from **hardcoded line ranges**. The registry's **51 was correct**. Definitive, marker-derived: **56 physical rows** − 3 retired-not-re-registered (C04–C06) − 1 promoted-out at v182 (C21) − 1 reclassified at v158 (C28) = **51 live**, split 38 N=1 + 12 N=2 + 1 N=4 (sums exactly). **After this audit's promotion: 50 live.** ⭐ **The instrument that settled it is the fix v254 and v255 both asked for and no ship executed: every row now carries a stable `C##` marker inside its first cell**, so counts are `grep -c` derivable and rows are citable by identifier. **This closes clause 7's standing WARN in the vault's inventory script.** ⚠️ Markers were **prefixed inside the first cell, not added as a column, because 13 of the 56 rows contain embedded pipes** — a field-splitting transform would have corrupted them (*before transforming a table programmatically, count its fields*).

**⭐⭐⭐ §C BIFURCATED — the four-times-deferred retire pass resolved by amending the rule.** §39 auto-retires an N=1 row past both floors (≥15 wikis AND ≥30 days); **23 live rows anchored pre-v187 are past both floors**, the oldest (C08, v132) by 126 ships — and v182, v203, v212 and every intervening ship declined to apply it. **The vault was carrying a declared-but-unenforced rule of exactly the kind this window documented in five subjects** (v250 six "non-negotiable" rules with one gate · v252 a block headed *"Automated, Zero Tolerance"* automating nothing · v256 a staleness mark declared, persisted, displayed, unenforced · v257 a policy declared three times and enforced at a fourth value · v258 an ignore rule deleted by the commit that added the files it named). **Resolution: the rule was wrong, not the practice.** §C now splits into **§C-1 live promotion candidates (N≥2, §39 applies) = 12** and **§C-2 recorded corpus-firsts (N=1, §39 does NOT apply) = 38**. **Zero rows destroyed** — the catalogue is what makes collision-detection possible (it is how v182 caught the #23 anchor error). A §C-2 row gaining a genuine 2nd instance is promoted into §C-1 and becomes subject to §39. ⚠️ **§28 RECALIBRATED:** the anti-inflation argument must be made against **§C-1 (12)**, not a 50-row catalogue, and **must not be the sole ground for declining a mint.** *(Reviewed at v259: no decline in the window rested on §28 alone.)* Membership index added above, machine-derivable.

**⭐⭐⭐ ROUTINE v2.8** — **§42** the same-author control adopted as method (the v256–v258 contribution: treat the comparison as the deliverable; separate **dispositions** from **attention**; verify every row with commands in *every* clone — both parent ships were corrected on exactly that error; an outcome replicating through *different mechanisms* is stronger than one repeating identically; state the sample size and refuse to generalise past it; do **not** mint a §C row for a method) · **§43** method rules **D31–D47** consolidated, plus **§43.1** the standing self-diagnosis (*reliable when reading and quoting a file, unreliable when generalising from where one chose to look* ⇒ every negative states its search extent), **§43.2** *a ground-truth block handed to a fleet is an AMPLIFIER* (v257: one unchecked assertion propagated into sixteen agents and returned as *"Established GT confirms…"*), **§43.3** fleet scope (*a wiki documents its subject; it does not repair it*) · **§44** the §C bifurcation above.

**BOOKKEEPING — verified, not inherited.** Streak arithmetic reconciles exactly: v212 `GA:72 · OG:11` → v213–v215 GA → **v216 OFF-GOAL** → v217–v218 GA → **v219 OFF-GOAL** → v220–v258 all GA ⇒ **`GA:116 · OG:13 [7 ov]`**, 72 + 44 = 116 across a 46-ship window with exactly 2 OFF-GOAL. **39 consecutive GA, v220→v258.** **Override review DISCHARGED, 11th consecutive** (v153→v258 = 0; lifetime 10, 3 logged `[ceiling-override]` v146/v148/v152). **§35 CLEAR** (window {v256, v257, v258} = 0 OG). **Top-level patterns 46 UNCHANGED, max #85 — no new top-level pattern in 46 ships.** **#18 B1-MCP ≈N=13 → ≈N=15** (v229, v230 first-party MCP servers; capability-vs-distribution ⇒ no double-count with #24). **#57 new widest fan-in record: 16 prior ships as entries in one subject** (v254, spanning v9→v215; prior record 10 at v253). **Five corpus-recursive REVISITS in-window** (v228 ↺v36 · v242 ↺v235 · v245 ↺v23, the first of an *outside-scope* anchor · v248 ↺v190 · v251-rebuild). **Model/Inference-Substrate tier → descriptive N=5** (+v246 needle).

Plus **new rule candidate D48:** line numbers from a `cat` of a glob are not file line numbers.

⭐⭐⭐ **RECORDED FOR THE AUDIT — §42 AMENDMENT CANDIDATE.** The N=4 run of the same-author habit table **broke four of six rows**, and the mechanism is a variable §42 does not currently record: habits that **accumulate in a working directory** (committed bytecode, committed machine paths, dead generations, and — via an 8-minute README — unbenchmarked headline claims) are **structurally unobservable in a bulk import**. This repository's `.gitignore` preceded its first `.py` file by 79 minutes because the code was finished before the repository existed. ⇒ **the control must record, per repository, whether the repository was the WORKSPACE or a DESTINATION; reading the absence of an accumulation habit as discipline mistakes workflow for character.** What survived at N=4 are the two habits independent of where the code was written: **run-instructions that describe a different program (4 repos, 4 distinct mechanisms — §42 clause 3's strongest form) and the total absence of any verification (0 tests, 0 CI, 0 agent surface, N=4).** ⚠️ This **materially corrects the v257 bolded conclusion** (*"Not a learning curve…"*). **Recorded, not self-executed — amending a routine section is an audit act.**

**Secondary, not minted:** #19 19a (fourth `mranex` data-point; small-scale ecosystem-portfolio at N=4) · LV-C7 Tauri-desktop cluster data-point · #12 clean NEGATIVE same-author N=4 · #66 MIXED · #83 NEGATIVE (the migration is declared; that the superseded generation still ships is not) · **v251's FALSE SUCCESS class at N=2** by a new mechanism (`spawn()` reports that a process *started*, not that it is *running*). **Tier T5 Application**, reviewable — no agent facet at all.

**⭐⭐⭐ THE FINDING THAT MATTERS FOR THE PATTERN LIBRARY — v260's FALSE SUCCESS class at N=2, one layer up.** v260: the Rust shell printed *"Successfully launched Python API server"* on a `spawn()` while `CREATE_NO_WINDOW` removed the channel that would have shown the failure. v261: a commit message printed *"Done 11 phase + code review round 2"* while the same commit deleted the master plan, the goal document, the 149-line agent protocol, the evidence directory and all 14 phase specifications. **Same shape, two layers — assert the success, delete the verification channel.** Four independent layers assert verification here (a declared `pytest` dependency, `Agent.md` §6 *"verify the work before claiming completion"*, a published `Verification:` command naming two directories that never existed, and the claim itself); **zero perform it**, and **not one evidence record was ever written** (extent: all 122 paths on every ref).

**⭐⭐ AND THE COUNTERWEIGHT, WHICH IS WHY THIS IS NOT A SIMPLE OVERCLAIM:** a 27-agent fleet graded the delivered code against all 14 recovered specifications with an adversarial refuter per phase — **5 FULLY · 7 SUBSTANTIALLY · 1 PARTIALLY of 13 graded** (phase 11 UNGRADED, its agent returned nothing). **The work was substantially done.** The refuters overturned 13 claims, **all in the author's favour**. What failed was verification, not delivery — and the one gap that was *not* pre-authorised is a quotation: `phase/00_app_foundation.md:156`, under `## Acceptance Criteria`, reads **"Basic schema tests pass."**

**⭐⭐⭐ THE RULE THIS SHIP CONTRIBUTES:** *a plan that pre-authorises a shortcut creates a **debt**, and deleting the plan deletes the only record that the debt exists.* Demonstrated exactly: `master_plan.md` §17 permitted plaintext API-key storage **on condition of a written warning**; the shortcut was taken (`schemas/__init__.py:19`), the warning was never written (extent: 77 files, nine terms incl. the Vietnamese `cảnh báo` — zero hits), and §17 was deleted. Successor to v258's *"a plan document is a fence around exactly the surfaces it names"* — here the fence named the surface precisely and was then demolished.

**⭐ #83 NEW SPECIES:** a genuine deficiency disclosure that was **written and then erased**. **#12 clean NEGATIVE, N=5** (zero tests, zero CI across all five `mranex` repositories). **#66 MIXED, best of the five.** **Tier T5** with a real method/process facet.

⭐ **v260's §42 amendment candidate CONFIRMED and REFINED:** this repository was the **workspace for the specification** (commits 1–3) and a **destination for the code** (commit 4, one 95-file drop) — a third category the amendment did not anticipate, and why the plan survives in the pack while no `.pyc` was ever committed. ⇒ **record workspace/destination status PER ARTIFACT CLASS, not per repository.** **And the N=5 result: engineering quality improves measurably project over project (three defects recorded at v257/v258/v260 are fixed here, citably); verification and documentary honesty do not improve at all.**

⭐⭐ **METHOD RESULT for the audit:** every fleet agent that read files produced checkable claims; **the one stage that read only other agents' claims — the synthesis — produced all five of the fleet's factual errors**, including calling a delivered, correctly-packaged feature nonexistent (11 modules, a working `__main__.py`, three tkinter imports) and inventing a *"~85%"* and a *"29 of 34"* that no file contains. ⇒ **aggregation stages have no ground truth to be wrong against, and they are the stage a reader is most likely to quote. Point the adversary at the synthesis, not only at the findings.** Extends **v249**'s *"the contradiction stage only protects the stages it is pointed at"* with a structural mechanism.

⭐⭐⭐ **§C-2 EARNED ITS KEEP — THE FIRST LIVE DEMONSTRATION OF THE v259 ARGUMENT.** The v259 audit declined to retire 38 N=1 rows on the ground that *"retiring them would destroy the mechanism that makes collision-detection possible."* Here that mechanism did exactly its job: **C37 surfaced from a routine collision grep, its definition was read, a false N=2 was prevented, and the boundary is now recorded so no future reader re-derives it.** The catalogue paid for itself, in the third ship after the bifurcation. **Third consecutive §44 application in a ship (v260, v261, v262), behaving as designed.**

**⭐⭐⭐ THE FINDING FOR THE PATTERN LIBRARY — and it closes the six-ship arc.** The five-repository same-author control found one habit replicating without exception: zero tests, zero CI, zero agent surface. **This repository sits in the same namespace, same language context, same media-pipeline domain, same weeks — and has 9 test files, 116 assertions, 3 deliberately-invalid fixtures, 2 CI workflows and a Claude Code skill. And it was forked on/after 2026-04-29, after which four more `mranex` projects were created (v256 May 13, v258 ~May 24, v260 May 27, v261 May 30), every one with none of it.** ⇒ ⭐⭐⭐ **v258's rule extends SIDEWAYS: code does not even move forward from a repository you OWN. FORKING IS NOT ADOPTING.** ⚠️ Stated fairly — forking is not a commitment to adopt, so this is **structural, not a character finding**: ⭐⭐⭐ **a practice does not transfer by proximity; practices transfer by DOING, not by POSSESSING.** ⭐ **With v261: declaration is not enforcement (a protocol with no gate is decorative), and possession is not practice (an example without practice is inert).**

**⭐⭐⭐ AND THE CORRECTION THAT IS THE BETTER FINDING — a textbook v250 specimen.** My first pass rated its README the run's strongest claims result; **the fleet's claims audit refuted that and, verified item by item, was right.** 🔴 *"12 Smart Templates"* (asserted 4×, incl. `:201` *"Zod discriminated unions (12 template variants)"*) — **six exist** · 🔴 *"6 theme palettes"* named — **zero in `src/`, every hit in the two README files** · 🔴 a **Gemini thumbnail stage** in the Tech Stack table and the pipeline diagram — **absent** · 🔴 `voiceChunks`, **hyperframes quality gates**, `sns_post.txt` — all absent · 🔴 fonts wrong 2 of 3. ✅ **And exactly right:** *"44 unit tests"* (**44**; the fleet said 45 and was wrong), 1080×1920@30fps (**asserted inside a test**), the 3-tier SFX selector, every dependency version, the determinism thesis (**0 `Math.random()` in `src/`, 0 network calls in the render path, the single `new Date()` confined to a sidecar `meta.json`**). ⇒ ⭐⭐⭐ **EVERYTHING THE CODE CAN CHECK IS RIGHT; EVERYTHING ONLY THE PROSE ASSERTS IS INFLATED — and the repository's FINAL commit is titled `docs: refresh README tech sections to match current codebase`, so the reconciliation was performed deliberately and did not converge.** ⇒ **He built real gates and aimed every one at the CODE; the prose has no gate, so the prose is where the drift lives** (v250: *a gate's AIM, not its quality, decides what rots*). ⇒ ⭐⭐⭐ **TESTS DO NOT MAKE YOU HONEST. THEY MAKE YOU HONEST ABOUT THE THINGS THEY TEST.**

🔴 **And the defect, with a callback:** `"test": "vitest run --passWithNoTests"` — the flag makes `npm test` **exit 0 on an empty suite**, and `test.yml` runs exactly that. ⭐⭐⭐ **At v261 the fleet's synthesis invented this mechanism for pytest, where it is false; it is real here, opted into deliberately, in the one repository of the six that actually has tests.** ⇒ ⭐⭐ **a gate that CANNOT FAIL is worse than a gate nobody invokes** — v261's verification command fails loudly and was never run; this one cannot fail and runs on every push.

**SECONDARY:** ⭐ **#12 POSITIVE — the first in this run of six** (five consecutive NEGATIVEs, v256–v261) · **#19 19a** first Ho Quang Hai author · **#66 mostly POSITIVE** (`.env.example` committed, `.env` gitignored, MIT licence file, `nock` so TTS tests never hit live APIs; ⚠️ pre-1.0 `hyperframes ^0.4.34` behind a caret) · **#83 MIXED** (the Gemini step is called *"gracefully skipped if absent"* — a partial disclosure — but the Tech Stack table and diagram present it as existing) · **Tier T5 with a genuine agent-skill facet.** **NON-CLAIMS:** NOT #52 (**the upstream's figures are unmeasured — I fetched the fork's page**) · NOT #57 (HyperFrames is `heygen-com/hyperframes`, a substrate C37 names, not a corpus subject) · NOT world-first · NOT a C37 instance · NOT executed.

⚠️ **ATTRIBUTION — the operator supplied `mranex/ainovel-cli`, a FORK; all 200 commits are by voocel (185) and four others, zero by the forker.** ⇒ **D49** (*a namespace is not a claim about authorship*) **earned its N=2 in the ship immediately after it was minted** — two consecutive supplied URLs, two forks. ⭐ **Cheaper detector found here: `78fd97b Merge branch 'main' of github.com:voocel/ainovel-cli` — a merge commit's message often names the upstream, needing no network call.** ⚠️⚠️ **AND IT CORRECTED v262:** ⭐ **NEW RULE D50 — a fork's clone gives you a LOWER BOUND on the fork date (its HEAD date), never the fork date.** v263's HEAD is 2026-06-24, necessarily *after* all five of that account's own projects, so at least one fork **postdates** his own work in the same domain ⇒ **v262's "the example was in his own account the whole time" is withdrawn and "forking is not adopting" is walked back to a claim about the present state.** The v262 documents were corrected in place and the artifact republished to the same URL.

**Declined N=2 of §C row C38** (*"Loop-Engineering Codification Kit"*, anchored on **loop-engineering v189**). C38 requires *a named-methodology pattern collection + starters + readiness-scoring/audit CLIs for scheduled unattended **coding-agent** loops — maker/checker split · LOOP.md/STATE.md conventions · an L0→L3 autonomy ladder · **cross-harness***. This is a single Go application for novel writing with none of those; the one shared element — **token-budget governance with an 80% warn and a kill-switch, implemented as `WarnRatio: 0.8` + `HardStop`** — is **one bullet** of C38's definition, not the class ⇒ **ADJACENCY, NOT INSTANCE.**

⭐⭐⭐ **§C-2 PREVENTED A FALSE N=2 FOR THE SECOND CONSECUTIVE SHIP.** v262: C37's *"the coding agent IS the runtime; NO orchestrator"* clause. v263: C38's codification-kit-versus-application distinction. **The v259 bifurcation's stated justification — that retiring the N=1 catalogue *"would destroy the mechanism that makes collision-detection possible"* — now has a DEMONSTRATED mechanism at N=2, in consecutive ships, three and four ships after the amendment.** **Fourth consecutive §44 application in a ship (v260/v261/v262/v263), behaving as designed.** A fresh §C-2 mint was also declined on five grounds, decisively **domain-not-capability** and ⭐ **the differentiating primitive does not ship here** — `StopGuard`/`StopDecision` are types in `voocel/agentcore`, a separate library by the same author (**v242 D25** applied to a component).

**⭐⭐⭐ THE FINDING FOR THE PATTERN LIBRARY — the three-ship ladder is complete.** **403 test functions, 11,868 lines, and no CI has ever run them.** Extent: zero `go test`/`go vet`/`golangci` in `.github/`; only four `.yml` files have existed on any ref and the **content of every yml blob ever committed** contains no `go test`; no Makefile/justfile/Taskfile; **both workflows fire only on `push: tags: v*`** — nothing on a branch push, nothing on `pull_request`, and **seven PRs from four outside contributors merged with zero automated checks.** ⇒ **v261 = the claim without the test (0 tests, a `Verification:` command naming directories that never existed) · v262 = the gate that cannot fail (`npm test --passWithNoTests`) · v263 = the test without the gate (403 excellent functions, no invoker).** ⇒ ⭐⭐⭐ **v262's rule reaches its final form: tests don't make you honest — they make you honest about the things they test, AND ONLY IF SOMETHING RUNS THEM. A test that nothing invokes is documentation.** ⭐⭐⭐ **And the ship's sentence: the most carefully engineered agent-safety machinery in the run — a three-way StopGuard, a boundary-aware budget kill-switch, checkpoint-derived loop detection, timeout-killed alerting, an ordered eviction policy with a protected tail — in a repository where EVERY GATE WAS BUILT FOR THE AGENT AND NONE FOR THE CODE.**

**⭐⭐⭐ AND WHERE THE RUN'S ARC CLOSES — this one KEPT its reasoning document.** `docs/refactor-flow-driven.md`, retained at HEAD, diagnoses *"~90% 是纯路由（LLM 复述 reminder）"* (~90% of Coordinator turns are the model restating an instruction code had already computed) and *"每章 ~3500-7000 tokens 花在 Coordinator 决策上，95% 是冗余"*, with a target of *"总计 ~1000-1500 tokens"*. **v252 deleted its eval results in the commit claiming the win; v261 deleted a 676-line plan in the commit titled "Done 11 phase"; v263 wrote the analysis, kept it, and the code matches it.** ⇒ **The design document is the only reason any of it is checkable.** ⚠️ Do **not** repeat the fleet's *"~100 tokens"* or *"~$175"* — its own reviewer refuted both, and no file contains a dollar figure.

**⭐⭐ THE MOST TRANSFERABLE ARCHITECTURE IN THE RUN:** `host.go:32-34` declares *"Host 是运行时薄外壳…**不做任何调度决策**"* and `internal/host/flow/router.go` is a **160-line pure function, `Route(s State) *Instruction`, a numbered 1→12 decision table over persisted state** ⇒ **the agent does not choose what to do next; code chooses, from state already persisted for resumability, and the agent executes. Semantic work stays with the model; SEQUENCING does not** — and cases 2/4/5 explicitly **decline** to route when the decision genuinely belongs to the model. Plus `dispatcher.go:112-140`'s `trackRepeat`: equality on **`Agent+Task` only, excluding `Reason` "because Reason is human-facing helper text"**, firing **once**, **outside the lock**, and **reset on resume** ⇒ ⭐ **four distinct bugs avoided in twenty lines**, feeding `notify` (killed on timeout) for a **complete end-to-end unattended-safety chain**. ⭐ And the philosophy: **it does not hard-fail on a repeat — it injects the evidence and lets the model reason**, the same move as `StopGuard`'s `InjectMessage`.

**SECONDARY:** ⭐ **#19 ecosystem-portfolio data-point** — voocel authored `agentcore` + `litellm` + this app (⚠️ `voocel/litellm` is distinct from BerriAI's Python `litellm`) · **#12 MIXED, a NEW SPECIES** — tests strongly POSITIVE, CI-that-runs-them absent; prior ships were POSITIVE or NEGATIVE on both together, **this splits them** · **#66 MIXED** (`redact.go` on diagnostic export, timeout-killed alerting, MIT licence file — against two same-author deps, an `install.sh`, and unchecked community PRs) · **Tier T5** with a substantial harness/runtime facet. **NON-CLAIMS:** NOT #52 (upstream figures unmeasured — the fork's page was fetched) · **NOT #57** — the `agentcore` collision hits were **`PageAgentCore`** from page-agent v199, an unanchored substring (⭐ third consecutive ship with this trap, after `ScrollMode`→`llm` and `Plex`→`multiplexer`; each caught by checking **where** the matches were, not **how many**) · NOT world-first · NOT a C38 instance · NOT executed.

⭐⭐ **METHOD RESULT — the counterpart to both preceding ships.** At v261 the aggregating stage produced every error while file-readers were reliable; at v262 a file-reader overturned the orchestrator; **here one file-reader produced the ship's three best findings AND three fabrications in the same report, and the adversarial reviewer separated them** (a wrong `WithMaxTurns` citation, an unsupported *"~100 tokens"*, and an invented *"~$175"*). ⇒ **The value is not that agents are reliable — it is that a reader and a refuter disagree in checkable ways. Aim an adversary at every stage, then verify what survives.**

**Streak GA:119 → GA:120 · OG:13 [7 ov]** (43 consecutive GA, v220→v263); **§35 CLEAR** {v261 GA, v262 GA, v263 GA} = 0 OG; no override.
